===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/NotificationsAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.graphics.Color
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DiffUtil
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.NotificationItem
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemNotificationBinding
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone
import kotlin.math.abs

class NotificationsAdapter(
    private var items: List<NotificationItem> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (NotificationItem) -> Unit = {},
    private val onDeleteSwipe: (NotificationItem, View) -> Unit = { _, _ -> }
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<NotificationsAdapter.NotifViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).apply {
            timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        }
        private const val DELETE_THRESHOLD_RATIO = 0.35f
        private const val MAX_SWIPE_DP = 200f
        private const val SWIPE_START_DP = 10f  // минимальный порог для старта свайпа
    }

    fun setItems(newItems: List<NotificationItem>) {
        val old = items
        val diff = DiffUtil.calculateDiff(object : DiffUtil.Callback() {
            override fun getOldListSize() = old.size
            override fun getNewListSize() = newItems.size
            override fun areItemsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o].id == newItems[n].id
            override fun areContentsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o] == newItems[n]
        })
        items = newItems
        diff.dispatchUpdatesTo(this)
    }

    fun appendItems(newItems: List<NotificationItem>) {
        if (newItems.isEmpty()) return
        val start = items.size
        items = items + newItems
        notifyItemRangeInserted(start, newItems.size)
    }

    fun getItems(): List<NotificationItem> = items

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): NotifViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemNotificationBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return NotifViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: NotifViewHolder, position: Int) {
        holder.bind(items[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class NotifViewHolder(private val binding: ItemNotificationBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        private val density = binding.root.resources.displayMetrics.density
        private val maxSwipePx = MAX_SWIPE_DP * density
        private val swipeStartPx = SWIPE_START_DP * density

        init {
            setupSwipe()
        }

        fun bind(item: NotificationItem) {
            if (item.authorAvatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.notifAvatar.load(item.authorAvatar) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                }
            }

            if (item.type == "reply") {
                binding.notifText.text = "${item.authorName} ответил на ваш комментарий"
                if (item.commentText.isNotEmpty()) {
                    binding.notifCommentText.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    binding.notifCommentText.text = "«${item.commentText}»"
                } else {
                    binding.notifCommentText.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            } else {
                binding.notifText.text = "${item.authorName} опубликовал новое видео: «${item.title}»"
                binding.notifCommentText.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            binding.notifDate.text = formatTimeAgo(item.timestamp)

            if (item.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.notifThumb.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.notifThumb.load(item.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                }
            } else {
                binding.notifThumb.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
            }

            binding.notifRoot.setBackgroundColor(
                if (item.read) Color.TRANSPARENT else 0x1AFFFFFF
            )

            binding.notifRoot.translationX = 0f
        }

        @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
        private fun setupSwipe() {
            val card = binding.notifRoot
            var startX = 0f
            var startY = 0f
            var isSwiping = false
            var downTime = 0L

            card.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
                when (event.actionMasked) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        startX = event.rawX
                        startY = event.rawY
                        isSwiping = false
                        downTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                        // Не блокируем сразу — даём RecyclerView шанс на вертикальный скролл
                        false
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val dx = event.rawX - startX
                        val dy = event.rawY - startY

                        // Проверяем: горизонтальный ли свайп
                        if (!isSwiping) {
                            // Если палец сместился вправо больше чем на swipeStartPx
                            // и движение явно горизонтальное
                            if (dx > swipeStartPx && dx > abs(dy) * 1.5f) {
                                isSwiping = true
                                // Отключаем перехват у RecyclerView, чтобы он не мешал
                                card.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(true)
                            }
                        }

                        if (isSwiping) {
                            // Ограничиваем только по макс. ширине
                            card.translationX = dx.coerceIn(0f, maxSwipePx)
                            true
                        } else {
                            false
                        }
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                        if (isSwiping) {
                            val dx = card.translationX
                            val threshold = card.width * DELETE_THRESHOLD_RATIO
                            val item = items.getOrNull(bindingAdapterPosition)
                            if (dx > threshold && item != null) {
                                // Удаляем — свайпаем дальше за экран, потом onDeleteSwipe
                                card.animate()
                                    .translationX(card.width.toFloat())
                                    .alpha(0f)
                                    .setDuration(200)
                                    .withEndAction {
                                        onDeleteSwipe(item, card)
                                    }
                                    .start()
                            } else {
                                // Возвращаем
                                card.animate()
                                    .translationX(0f)
                                    .setDuration(180)
                                    .start()
                            }
                            card.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(false)
                            isSwiping = false
                            true
                        } else {
                            // Обычный тап — не свайпали
                            val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - downTime
                            if (elapsed < 400) {
                                val item = items.getOrNull(bindingAdapterPosition)
                                if (item != null) {
                                    onClick(item)
                                }
                            }
                            false
                        }
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                        if (isSwiping) {
                            card.animate()
                                .translationX(0f)
                                .setDuration(180)
                                .start()
                            card.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(false)
                            isSwiping = false
                        }
                        false
                    }
                    else -> false
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun formatTimeAgo(gmtDate: String): String {
        if (gmtDate.isEmpty()) return ""
        return try {
            val date = synchronized(dateFmt) { dateFmt.parse(gmtDate) } ?: return gmtDate
            val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
            when {
                diff < 60 -> "только что"
                diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60} мин назад"
                diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600} ч назад"
                diff < 2_592_000 -> "${diff / 86400} дн назад"
                diff < 31_536_000 -> "${diff / 2_592_000} мес назад"
                else -> "${diff / 31_536_000} г назад"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            gmtDate
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/DownloadsFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.TextView
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentDownloadsBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.download.DownloadStorage
import ru.asmr1.app.download.DownloadedFile
import ru.asmr1.app.download.Downloader

class DownloadsFragment : Fragment() {

    private var _binding: FragmentDownloadsBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: DownloadsAdapter
    private lateinit var storage: DownloadStorage
    private lateinit var downloader: Downloader

    // Фильтры
    private var typeFilter: DownloadedFile.Type? = null  // null = все
    private var sortDesc: Boolean = true                 // true = ↓ новые

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentDownloadsBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        storage = DownloadStorage(requireContext())
        downloader = Downloader(requireContext())

        setupRecycler()
        setupChips()
        loadDownloads()
    }

    private fun setupRecycler() {
        adapter = DownloadsAdapter(
            onClick = { item ->
                // Открываем плеер — он сам определит локальный/онлайн
                (requireActivity() as? MainActivity)?.openPlayer(item.toVideo())
            },
            onDelete = { item ->
                confirmDelete(item)
            }
        )

        binding.recyclerView.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.recyclerView.adapter = adapter
        binding.recyclerView.setHasFixedSize(false)
        binding.recyclerView.itemAnimator = null
    }

    private fun setupChips() {
        binding.chipAll.setOnClickListener {
            typeFilter = null
            updateChipsUI()
            loadDownloads()
        }
        binding.chipVideo.setOnClickListener {
            typeFilter = DownloadedFile.Type.VIDEO
            updateChipsUI()
            loadDownloads()
        }
        binding.chipAudio.setOnClickListener {
            typeFilter = DownloadedFile.Type.AUDIO
            updateChipsUI()
            loadDownloads()
        }
        binding.chipOrder.setOnClickListener {
            sortDesc = !sortDesc
            updateChipsUI()
            loadDownloads()
        }

        updateChipsUI()
    }

    private fun updateChipsUI() {
        _binding?.apply {
            setChipActive(chipAll, typeFilter == null)
            setChipActive(chipVideo, typeFilter == DownloadedFile.Type.VIDEO)
            setChipActive(chipAudio, typeFilter == DownloadedFile.Type.AUDIO)
            chipOrder.text = if (sortDesc) "↓ Новые" else "↑ Старые"
            setChipActive(chipOrder, false)
        }
    }

    private fun setChipActive(chip: TextView, active: Boolean) {
        chip.isSelected = active
        chip.setTextColor(
            ContextCompat.getColor(
                requireContext(),
                if (active) android.R.color.white else R.color.text_secondary
            )
        )
    }

    private fun loadDownloads() {
        val list = storage.getDownloads(typeFilter).let {
            if (sortDesc) it.sortedByDescending { d -> d.downloadedAt }
            else it.sortedBy { d -> d.downloadedAt }
        }

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            binding.recyclerView.visibility = View.GONE
            binding.llEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        } else {
            binding.recyclerView.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            binding.llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
        }

        adapter.setItems(list)
    }

    private fun confirmDelete(item: DownloadedFile) {
        val typeName = if (item.type == DownloadedFile.Type.VIDEO) "видео" else "аудио"
        androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
            .setTitle("Удалить?")
            .setMessage("Удалить скачанное $typeName «${item.title}»?")
            .setPositiveButton("Удалить") { _, _ ->
                downloader.delete(item.videoId, item.type)
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Удалено", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                loadDownloads()
                // Обновляем фрагмент — таб «Скачано» может скрыться, если ничего не осталось
                (activity as? MainActivity)?.supportFragmentManager
            }
            .setNegativeButton("Отмена", null)
            .show()
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        // При возврате — обновляем список (вдруг что-то удалили снаружи)
        if (_binding != null) loadDownloads()
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/VideoListFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.util.Log
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import androidx.viewpager2.widget.ViewPager2
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialog
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentVideoListBinding

class VideoListFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        const val ARG_TAB = "tab"
        val sharedPool: RecyclerView.RecycledViewPool by lazy { RecyclerView.RecycledViewPool() }
        fun newInstance(tab: String) = VideoListFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putString(ARG_TAB, tab) }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentVideoListBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: VideoAdapter
    private lateinit var layoutManager: LinearLayoutManager
    private var parentPager: ViewPager2? = null

    private var currentTab = "recommended"
    private var currentPage = 1
    private var isLoading = false
    private var hasMore = true
    private val perPage = 10
    private var loadJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentVideoListBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        currentTab = arguments?.getString(ARG_TAB) ?: "recommended"
        parentPager = findParentViewPager2(view)

        setupRecyclerView()
        setupSwipeRefresh()
        setupInfiniteScroll()
        loadFeed(1, false)
    }

    private fun findParentViewPager2(view: View): ViewPager2? {
        var v: View? = view.parent as? View
        while (v != null) {
            if (v is ViewPager2) return v
            v = v.parent as? View
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun setupRecyclerView() {
        adapter = VideoAdapter(
            onClick = { video ->
                (requireActivity() as ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity).openPlayer(video)
            },
            onMoreClick = { video, _ -> showVideoMenu(video) }
        )
        layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.recyclerView.layoutManager = layoutManager
        binding.recyclerView.adapter = adapter

        binding.recyclerView.setHasFixedSize(false)
        binding.recyclerView.itemAnimator = null
        binding.recyclerView.setItemViewCacheSize(30)
        binding.recyclerView.setRecycledViewPool(sharedPool)
        binding.recyclerView.isNestedScrollingEnabled = true
        layoutManager.isItemPrefetchEnabled = true
        layoutManager.initialPrefetchItemCount = 10

        binding.recyclerView.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrollStateChanged(recyclerView: RecyclerView, newState: Int) {
                when (newState) {
                    RecyclerView.SCROLL_STATE_DRAGGING,
                    RecyclerView.SCROLL_STATE_SETTLING -> {
                        parentPager?.isUserInputEnabled = false
                    }
                    RecyclerView.SCROLL_STATE_IDLE -> {
                        parentPager?.isUserInputEnabled = true
                    }
                }
            }
        })
    }

    fun scrollToTop() {
        _binding?.recyclerView?.scrollToPosition(0)
    }

    private fun showVideoMenu(video: Video) {
        try {
            val sheetView = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.sheet_video_menu, null)
            val dialog = BottomSheetDialog(requireContext())
            dialog.setContentView(sheetView)
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_hide)?.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_hide_channel)?.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_save)?.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_report)?.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_cancel)?.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
            dialog.show()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("VideoListFragment", "showVideoMenu failed", e)
        }
    }

    private fun setupSwipeRefresh() {
        binding.swipeRefresh.setOnRefreshListener {
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
            loadFeed(1, false)
        }
    }

    private fun setupInfiniteScroll() {
        binding.recyclerView.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(recyclerView: RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                super.onScrolled(recyclerView, dx, dy)
                val last = layoutManager.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (last > 0) adapter.prefetchAround(recyclerView.context, last)
                if (dy <= 0) return
                if (isLoading || !hasMore) return
                if (_binding == null) return

                val totalItems = layoutManager.itemCount
                val lastVisible = layoutManager.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (totalItems > 0 && lastVisible >= totalItems - 10) {
                    currentPage++
                    loadFeed(currentPage, true)
                }
            }
        })
    }

    private fun loadFeed(page: Int, append: Boolean) {
        if (isLoading) return
        isLoading = true
        if (!append) {
            if (_binding != null) {
                if (adapter.itemCount == 0) adapter.showSkeleton()
                else binding.swipeRefresh.isRefreshing = true
            }
        }
        loadJob?.cancel()
        loadJob = lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val response = ApiClient.service.getFeed(tab = currentTab, page = page, perPage = perPage)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                if (response.success) {
                    if (append) adapter.appendVideos(response.videos)
                    else adapter.setVideos(response.videos)
                    hasMore = response.hasMore
                } else {
                    adapter.setVideos(emptyList())
                    hasMore = false
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e("VideoListFragment", "Ошибка загрузки", e)
                if (_binding != null) adapter.setVideos(emptyList())
                hasMore = false
            } finally {
                isLoading = false
                _binding?.swipeRefresh?.isRefreshing = false
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        parentPager?.isUserInputEnabled = true
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/SearchHistoryAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemSearchHistoryBinding

class SearchHistoryAdapter(
    private var items: List<String> = emptyList(),
    private val onItemClick: (String) -> Unit,
    private val onItemDelete: (String) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<SearchHistoryAdapter.HistoryViewHolder>() {

    fun setItems(newItems: List<String>) {
        items = newItems
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): HistoryViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemSearchHistoryBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return HistoryViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: HistoryViewHolder, position: Int) {
        holder.bind(items[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class HistoryViewHolder(private val binding: ItemSearchHistoryBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(query: String) {
            binding.tvQuery.text = query
            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onItemClick(query) }
            binding.btnDelete.setOnClickListener { onItemDelete(query) }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/VideoAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DiffUtil
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.imageLoader
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import coil.request.ImageRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemVideoBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemVideoSkeletonBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.formatDuration
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

class VideoAdapter(
    private var videos: List<Video> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (Video) -> Unit = {},
    private val onMoreClick: (Video, View) -> Unit = { _, _ -> },
    private val hideAuthor: Boolean = false
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<RecyclerView.ViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private const val TYPE_VIDEO = 0
        private const val TYPE_SKELETON = 1
        private const val SKELETON_COUNT = 5
        private const val PREFETCH_AHEAD = 2

        private val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).apply {
            timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        }
    }

    private var showSkeleton = false

    fun setVideos(newVideos: List<Video>) {
        val wasSkeleton = showSkeleton
        showSkeleton = false

        if (wasSkeleton) {
            videos = newVideos
            notifyDataSetChanged()
            return
        }

        val old = videos
        val diff = DiffUtil.calculateDiff(object : DiffUtil.Callback() {
            override fun getOldListSize() = old.size
            override fun getNewListSize() = newVideos.size
            override fun areItemsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o].id == newVideos[n].id
            override fun areContentsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o] == newVideos[n]
        })
        videos = newVideos
        diff.dispatchUpdatesTo(this)
    }

    fun appendVideos(newVideos: List<Video>) {
        if (newVideos.isEmpty()) return
        val startPos = videos.size
        videos = videos + newVideos
        notifyItemRangeInserted(startPos, newVideos.size)
    }

    fun showSkeleton() {
        showSkeleton = true
        videos = emptyList()
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun getItemViewType(position: Int): Int =
        if (showSkeleton) TYPE_SKELETON else TYPE_VIDEO

    override fun getItemCount(): Int =
        if (showSkeleton) SKELETON_COUNT else videos.size

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        val inflater = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
        return when (viewType) {
            TYPE_SKELETON -> SkeletonViewHolder(
                ItemVideoSkeletonBinding.inflate(inflater, parent, false)
            )
            else -> {
                if (hideAuthor) {
                    HorizontalAuthorViewHolder(
                        ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemVideoHorizontalAuthorBinding
                            .inflate(inflater, parent, false)
                    )
                } else {
                    VideoViewHolder(
                        ItemVideoBinding.inflate(inflater, parent, false)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: RecyclerView.ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        when (holder) {
            is VideoViewHolder -> holder.bind(videos[position])
            is HorizontalAuthorViewHolder -> holder.bind(videos[position])
            is SkeletonViewHolder -> holder.bind()
        }
    }

    fun prefetchAround(context: android.content.Context, anchorPosition: Int) {
        if (showSkeleton) return
        val loader = context.imageLoader
        for (i in 1..PREFETCH_AHEAD) {
            val idx = anchorPosition + i
            if (idx >= videos.size) break
            val v = videos[idx]
            if (v.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                loader.enqueue(
                    ImageRequest.Builder(context)
                        .data(v.thumbnail)
                        .memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                        .diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                        .build()
                )
            }
        }
    }

    inner class VideoViewHolder(private val binding: ItemVideoBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(video: Video) {
            binding.title.text = video.title
            binding.meta.text = video.author.name

            // На странице автора скрываем аватар и ник автора — они лишние
            if (hideAuthor) {
                binding.authorAvatar.visibility = View.GONE
                binding.meta.visibility = View.GONE
            } else {
                binding.authorAvatar.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.meta.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            }

            binding.viewsOverlay.text = formatViewsShort(video.views)
            binding.dateOverlay.text = timeAgoShort(video.published)

            binding.thumbnail.transitionName = "video_thumb_" + video.id

            if (video.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.thumbnail.load(video.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            if (video.author.avatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.authorAvatar.load(video.author.avatar) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            if (video.duration > 0) {
                binding.duration.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.duration.text = formatDuration(video.duration)
            } else {
                binding.duration.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            binding.root.setOnClickListener {
                val rect = android.graphics.Rect()
                binding.thumbnail.getGlobalVisibleRect(rect)
                onClick(video)
            }
            binding.btnMore.setOnClickListener { v -> onMoreClick(video, v) }
            // Аватар автора → страница автора
            binding.authorAvatar.setOnClickListener {
                val act = itemView.context as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
                act?.openAuthorPage(video.author.id)
            }

            // Ник автора → страница автора
            binding.meta.setOnClickListener {
                val act = itemView.context as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
                act?.openAuthorPage(video.author.id)
            }

        }
    }

    class SkeletonViewHolder(private val binding: ItemVideoSkeletonBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind() {
            binding.veilLayout.veil()
        }
    }

    inner class HorizontalAuthorViewHolder(
        private val binding: ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemVideoHorizontalAuthorBinding
    ) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(video: Video) {
            binding.title.text = video.title
            binding.viewsOverlay.text = formatViewsShort(video.views)
            binding.dateOverlay.text = timeAgoShort(video.published)

            if (video.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.thumbnail.load(video.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(video) }
            binding.btnMore.setOnClickListener { v -> onMoreClick(video, v) }
        }
    }

    private fun formatViewsShort(n: Int): String = when {
        n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fM", n / 1_000_000.0)
        n >= 1_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fK", n / 1_000.0)
        else -> n.toString()
    }

    private fun timeAgoShort(dateStr: String): String {
        if (dateStr.isEmpty()) return ""
        return try {
            val date = synchronized(dateFmt) { dateFmt.parse(dateStr) } ?: return ""
            val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
            when {
                diff < 60 -> "сейчас"
                diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60}м"
                diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600}ч"
                diff < 2_592_000 -> "${diff / 86400}д"
                diff < 31_536_000 -> "${diff / 2_592_000}мес"
                else -> "${diff / 31_536_000}г"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            ""
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/SearchFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.text.Editable
import android.text.TextWatcher
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.inputmethod.EditorInfo
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.TextView
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentSearchBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.SearchHistoryManager

class SearchFragment : Fragment() {

    private var _binding: FragmentSearchBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: SearchAdapter
    private lateinit var historyAdapter: SearchHistoryAdapter
    private lateinit var layoutManager: LinearLayoutManager

    private lateinit var historyManager: SearchHistoryManager

    private var currentQuery = ""
    private var currentPage = 1
    private var isLoading = false
    private var hasMore = true
    private val perPage = 20

    // Сортировка
    private var orderBy: String = "weight"
    private var order: String = "DESC"

    private var searchJob: Job? = null
    private var searchDebounceJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentSearchBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        historyManager = SearchHistoryManager(requireContext())

        setupRecyclerView()
        setupHistoryRecycler()
        setupSearchInput()
        setupButtons()
        setupSortChips()

        showInitialState()

        binding.etSearch.postDelayed({
            if (_binding == null) return@postDelayed
            binding.etSearch.requestFocus()
            val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
            imm?.showSoftInput(binding.etSearch, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
        }, 200)
    }

    private fun setupRecyclerView() {
        adapter = SearchAdapter { video ->
            (requireActivity() as? MainActivity)?.openPlayer(video)
        }
        layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.recyclerView.layoutManager = layoutManager
        binding.recyclerView.adapter = adapter
        binding.recyclerView.setHasFixedSize(false)
        binding.recyclerView.itemAnimator = null

        binding.recyclerView.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(recyclerView: RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                super.onScrolled(recyclerView, dx, dy)
                if (dy <= 0) return
                if (isLoading || !hasMore) return
                if (_binding == null) return

                val totalItems = layoutManager.itemCount
                val lastVisible = layoutManager.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (totalItems > 0 && lastVisible >= totalItems - 5) {
                    currentPage++
                    performSearch(currentQuery, currentPage, append = true)
                }
            }
        })

        binding.swipeRefresh.setOnRefreshListener {
            if (currentQuery.isNotEmpty()) {
                currentPage = 1
                hasMore = true
                performSearch(currentQuery, 1, append = false)
            } else {
                binding.swipeRefresh.isRefreshing = false
            }
        }
    }

    private fun setupHistoryRecycler() {
        historyAdapter = SearchHistoryAdapter(
            items = emptyList(),
            onItemClick = { query ->
                binding.etSearch.setText(query)
                binding.etSearch.setSelection(query.length)
                currentQuery = query
                currentPage = 1
                hasMore = true
                performSearch(query, 1, append = false)
                hideKeyboard()
            },
            onItemDelete = { query ->
                historyManager.removeQuery(query)
                refreshHistory()
            }
        )
        binding.rvHistory.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.rvHistory.adapter = historyAdapter
    }

    private fun setupSearchInput() {
        binding.etSearch.addTextChangedListener(object : TextWatcher {
            override fun beforeTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, start: Int, count: Int, after: Int) {}
            override fun onTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, start: Int, before: Int, count: Int) {
                val hasText = !s.isNullOrEmpty()
                binding.btnClear.visibility = if (hasText) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

                val query = s?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()

                if (query.isEmpty()) {
                    showInitialState()
                    return
                }

                searchDebounceJob?.cancel()
                searchDebounceJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
                    delay(500)
                    currentQuery = query
                    currentPage = 1
                    hasMore = true
                    performSearch(query, 1, append = false)
                }
            }
            override fun afterTextChanged(s: Editable?) {}
        })

        binding.etSearch.setOnFocusChangeListener { _, hasFocus ->
            if (hasFocus && binding.etSearch.text.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                showInitialState()
            }
        }

        binding.etSearch.setOnEditorActionListener { _, actionId, _ ->
            if (actionId == EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_SEARCH) {
                searchDebounceJob?.cancel()
                val query = binding.etSearch.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
                if (query.isNotEmpty()) {
                    historyManager.addQuery(query)
                    currentQuery = query
                    currentPage = 1
                    hasMore = true
                    performSearch(query, 1, append = false)
                    hideKeyboard()
                }
                true
            } else false
        }
    }

    private fun setupButtons() {
        binding.btnSearch.setOnClickListener {
            searchDebounceJob?.cancel()
            val query = binding.etSearch.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
            if (query.isNotEmpty()) {
                historyManager.addQuery(query)
                currentQuery = query
                currentPage = 1
                hasMore = true
                performSearch(query, 1, append = false)
                hideKeyboard()
            } else {
                Toast.makeText(context, "Введите запрос", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }

        binding.btnClear.setOnClickListener {
            binding.etSearch.text?.clear()
            adapter.clear()
            showInitialState()
        }

        binding.btnClearHistory.setOnClickListener {
            historyManager.clearAll()
            showInitialState()
        }
    }

    // ===== ЧИПЫ СОРТИРОВКИ =====
    private fun setupSortChips() {
        binding.chipWeight.setOnClickListener { selectOrderBy("weight") }
        binding.chipDate.setOnClickListener { selectOrderBy("date") }
        binding.chipViews.setOnClickListener { selectOrderBy("views") }
        binding.chipLikes.setOnClickListener { selectOrderBy("likes") }
        binding.chipTitle.setOnClickListener { selectOrderBy("title") }

        binding.chipOrder.setOnClickListener {
            order = if (order == "DESC") "ASC" else "DESC"
            updateOrderChipText()
            applySortAndSearch()
        }

        updateSortChipsUI()
        updateOrderChipText()
    }

    private fun selectOrderBy(newOrderBy: String) {
        if (orderBy == newOrderBy) return
        orderBy = newOrderBy
        updateSortChipsUI()
        applySortAndSearch()
    }

    private fun updateSortChipsUI() {
        _binding?.apply {
            setChipActive(chipWeight, orderBy == "weight")
            setChipActive(chipDate, orderBy == "date")
            setChipActive(chipViews, orderBy == "views")
            setChipActive(chipLikes, orderBy == "likes")
            setChipActive(chipTitle, orderBy == "title")
        }
    }

    private fun setChipActive(chip: TextView, active: Boolean) {
        chip.isSelected = active
        chip.setTextColor(
            ContextCompat.getColor(
                requireContext(),
                if (active) android.R.color.white else R.color.text_secondary
            )
        )
    }

    private fun updateOrderChipText() {
        _binding?.chipOrder?.text = if (order == "DESC") "↓ Убыв." else "↑ Возр."
    }

    /** Если есть активный запрос — перезапускаем поиск с новой сортировкой */
    private fun applySortAndSearch() {
        val query = binding.etSearch.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (query.isNotEmpty()) {
            currentQuery = query
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
            performSearch(query, 1, append = false)
        }
    }

    private fun performSearch(query: String, page: Int, append: Boolean) {
        if (isLoading) return
        if (query.isEmpty()) return

        isLoading = true

        if (!append) {
            showSkeletons()
            _binding?.apply {
                llHistory.visibility = View.GONE
                llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
                llNoResults.visibility = View.GONE
                swipeRefresh.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                llSortBar.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            }
        }

        searchJob?.cancel()
        searchJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val response = ApiClient.service.search(
                    query = query,
                    page = page,
                    perPage = perPage,
                    orderBy = orderBy,
                    order = order
                )

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    if (append) {
                        adapter.appendVideos(response.videos)
                    } else {
                        adapter.setVideos(response.videos)
                    }
                    hasMore = response.hasMore

                    if (response.videos.isEmpty()) {
                        showNoResults(query)
                    } else {
                        showResults()
                    }
                } else {
                    if (!append) showNoResults(query)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                Toast.makeText(context, "Ошибка поиска: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                if (!append) showNoResults(query)
            } finally {
                isLoading = false
                _binding?.swipeRefresh?.isRefreshing = false
            }
        }
    }

    private fun showInitialState() {
        _binding?.apply {
            swipeRefresh.visibility = View.GONE
            llNoResults.visibility = View.GONE
            llSortBar.visibility = View.GONE

            val history = historyManager.getHistory()
            if (history.isNotEmpty()) {
                historyAdapter.setItems(history)
                llHistory.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
            } else {
                llHistory.visibility = View.GONE
                llEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            }
        }
    }

    private fun refreshHistory() {
        val history = historyManager.getHistory()
        if (history.isEmpty()) {
            _binding?.llHistory?.visibility = View.GONE
            _binding?.llEmpty?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        } else {
            historyAdapter.setItems(history)
        }
    }

    private fun showSkeletons() {
        adapter.showSkeletons()
    }

    private fun showResults() {
        _binding?.apply {
            llHistory.visibility = View.GONE
            llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
            llNoResults.visibility = View.GONE
            swipeRefresh.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            llSortBar.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
    }

    private fun showNoResults(query: String) {
        _binding?.apply {
            llHistory.visibility = View.GONE
            llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
            swipeRefresh.visibility = View.GONE
            llSortBar.visibility = View.GONE
            llNoResults.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            tvNoResults.text = "По запросу «$query» ничего не найдено"
        }
    }

    private fun hideKeyboard() {
        val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(
            requireContext(),
            InputMethodManager::class.java
        )
        imm?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(binding.etSearch.windowToken, 0)
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        searchJob?.cancel()
        searchDebounceJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/SubscriptionsAuthorsAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.SubscribedAuthor
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemSubscribedAllBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemSubscribedAuthorBinding

class SubscriptionsAuthorsAdapter(
    private val onAllClick: () -> Unit,
    private val onAuthorClick: (SubscribedAuthor) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<RecyclerView.ViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private const val TYPE_ALL = 0
        private const val TYPE_AUTHOR = 1
    }

    private var authors: List<SubscribedAuthor> = emptyList()
    private var selectedAuthorId: Int = 0

    fun setAuthors(newAuthors: List<SubscribedAuthor>) {
        authors = newAuthors
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    fun setSelected(authorId: Int) {
        selectedAuthorId = authorId
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    fun markAuthorAsViewed(authorId: Int) {
        val index = authors.indexOfFirst { it.id == authorId }
        if (index < 0) return
        val current = authors[index]
        if (!current.hasNew) return
        val updated = current.copy(hasNew = false)
        authors = authors.toMutableList().apply { set(index, updated) }
        notifyItemChanged(index + 1)
    }

    override fun getItemViewType(position: Int): Int =
        if (position == 0) TYPE_ALL else TYPE_AUTHOR

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = authors.size + 1

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        val inflater = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
        return when (viewType) {
            TYPE_ALL -> AllViewHolder(
                ItemSubscribedAllBinding.inflate(inflater, parent, false)
            )
            else -> AuthorViewHolder(
                ItemSubscribedAuthorBinding.inflate(inflater, parent, false)
            )
        }
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: RecyclerView.ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        when (holder) {
            is AllViewHolder -> holder.bind()
            is AuthorViewHolder -> holder.bind(authors[position - 1])
        }
    }

    inner class AllViewHolder(private val binding: ItemSubscribedAllBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {
        fun bind() {
            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onAllClick() }
            binding.root.alpha = if (selectedAuthorId == 0) 1f else 0.6f
        }
    }

    inner class AuthorViewHolder(private val binding: ItemSubscribedAuthorBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(author: SubscribedAuthor) {
            binding.tvName.text = author.name

            if (author.avatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.ivAvatar.load(author.avatar) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            binding.vNewRing.visibility = if (author.hasNew) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

            val isSelected = selectedAuthorId == author.id
            binding.root.alpha = if (isSelected || selectedAuthorId == 0) 1f else 0.6f

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onAuthorClick(author) }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/NotificationsTabFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Author
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Comment
import ru.asmr1.app.data.NotificationItem
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentNotificationsTabBinding

class NotificationsTabFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_FILTER = "filter"
        private const val ARG_UNREAD = "unread_only"
        fun newInstance(filter: String, unreadOnly: Boolean) = NotificationsTabFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply {
                putString(ARG_FILTER, filter)
                putBoolean(ARG_UNREAD, unreadOnly)
            }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentNotificationsTabBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: NotificationsAdapter
    private lateinit var layoutManager: LinearLayoutManager
    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager

    private var filter: String = "all"
    private var unreadOnly: Boolean = false
    private var currentPage = 1
    private var isLoading = false
    private var hasMore = true

    private var loadJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentNotificationsTabBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())
        filter = arguments?.getString(ARG_FILTER) ?: "all"
        unreadOnly = arguments?.getBoolean(ARG_UNREAD, false) ?: false

        setupRecycler()
        load(reset = true)
    }

    private fun setupRecycler() {
        adapter = NotificationsAdapter(
            onClick = { item -> onItemClick(item) },
            onDeleteSwipe = { item, viewToAnimate -> deleteItem(item, viewToAnimate) }
        )
        layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.notifRecycler.layoutManager = layoutManager
        binding.notifRecycler.adapter = adapter
        binding.notifRecycler.itemAnimator = null

        binding.notifRecycler.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(rv: RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                super.onScrolled(rv, dx, dy)
                if (dy <= 0) return
                if (isLoading || !hasMore) return
                if (_binding == null) return
                val total = layoutManager.itemCount
                val last = layoutManager.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (total > 0 && last >= total - 3) {
                    currentPage++
                    load(reset = false)
                }
            }
        })
    }

    fun reload() {
        currentPage = 1
        hasMore = true
        adapter.setItems(emptyList())
        load(reset = true)
    }

    private fun load(reset: Boolean) {
        if (isLoading) return
        if (!hasMore && !reset) return
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            binding.notifEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            return
        }

        if (reset) {
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
        } else {
            binding.notifLoading.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
        isLoading = true

        loadJob?.cancel()
        loadJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                val response = api.getNotifications(
                    filter = filter,
                    page = currentPage,
                    perPage = 20
                )

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    val items = if (unreadOnly) {
                        response.items.filter { !it.read }
                    } else {
                        response.items
                    }

                    if (reset) {
                        adapter.setItems(items)
                    } else {
                        adapter.appendItems(items)
                    }
                    hasMore = response.hasMore

                    binding.notifEmpty.visibility =
                        if (items.isEmpty() && reset) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
                } else {
                    hasMore = false
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null && reset) binding.notifEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            } finally {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    isLoading = false
                    binding.notifLoading.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun deleteItem(item: NotificationItem, viewToAnimate: View) {
        // Сразу убираем из списка (визуально)
        val newList = adapter.getItems().filterNot { it.id == item.id }
        adapter.setItems(newList)
        if (newList.isEmpty()) binding.notifEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE

        // В фоне — на сервер
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                ApiClient.createJwtService(token).deleteNotification(item.id)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    private fun onItemClick(item: NotificationItem) {
        // Локально помечаем прочитанным сразу
        val updatedList = adapter.getItems().map {
            if (it.id == item.id) it.copy(read = true) else it
        }
        adapter.setItems(updatedList)

        // В фоне отправляем на сервер
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                ApiClient.createJwtService(token).markNotificationRead(item.id)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        val activity = activity as? MainActivity ?: return

        val video = Video(
            id = item.postId,
            title = item.title,
            description = "",
            thumbnail = item.thumbnail,
            videoUrl = item.videoUrl ?: "",
            audioUrl = item.audioUrl,
            duration = 0,
            views = 0,
            likes = 0,
            published = item.timestamp,
            author = Author(
                id = item.authorId,
                name = item.authorName,
                avatar = item.authorAvatar
            ),
            tags = null
        )

        if (item.type == "reply" && item.parentCommentId > 0) {
            val stubComment = Comment(
                id = item.parentCommentId,
                content = "",
                authorId = 0,
                authorName = "",
                authorAvatar = "",
                date = "",
                dateGmt = "",
                likes = 0,
                liked = false,
                parentId = 0,
                repliesCount = 0,
                replies = emptyList()
            )
            activity.openPlayer(video)
            activity.openCommentsWithFocus(video, stubComment)
        } else {
            activity.openPlayer(video)
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/SubscriptionsFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentSubscriptionsBinding

class SubscriptionsFragment : Fragment() {

    private var _binding: FragmentSubscriptionsBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var authorsAdapter: SubscriptionsAuthorsAdapter
    private lateinit var videosAdapter: VideoAdapter
    private lateinit var videosLayoutManager: LinearLayoutManager
    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager

    private var selectedAuthorId: Int = 0
    private var currentPage = 1
    private var isLoading = false
    private var hasMore = true
    private val perPage = 20

    private var loadJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentSubscriptionsBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            showEmptyState()
            return
        }

        setupAuthorsRecycler()
        setupVideosRecycler()
        setupSwipeRefresh()

        loadAuthors()
        loadVideos(reset = true)
    }

    private fun setupAuthorsRecycler() {
        authorsAdapter = SubscriptionsAuthorsAdapter(
            onAllClick = {
                selectedAuthorId = 0
                authorsAdapter.setSelected(0)
                loadVideos(reset = true)
            },
            onAuthorClick = { author ->
                selectedAuthorId = author.id
                authorsAdapter.setSelected(author.id)
                loadVideos(reset = true)
            }
        )
        binding.rvAuthors.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(
            requireContext(), LinearLayoutManager.HORIZONTAL, false
        )
        binding.rvAuthors.adapter = authorsAdapter
    }

    private fun setupVideosRecycler() {
        videosAdapter = VideoAdapter(
            onClick = { video ->
                markAuthorAsViewed(video.author.id)
                (requireActivity() as? MainActivity)?.openPlayer(video)
            },
            onMoreClick = { _, _ -> }
        )
        videosLayoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.rvVideos.layoutManager = videosLayoutManager
        binding.rvVideos.adapter = videosAdapter
        binding.rvVideos.setHasFixedSize(false)
        binding.rvVideos.itemAnimator = null

        binding.rvVideos.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(recyclerView: RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                super.onScrolled(recyclerView, dx, dy)
                if (dy <= 0) return
                if (isLoading || !hasMore) return
                if (_binding == null) return

                val total = videosLayoutManager.itemCount
                val lastVisible = videosLayoutManager.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (total > 0 && lastVisible >= total - 5) {
                    currentPage++
                    loadVideos(reset = false)
                }
            }
        })
    }

    private fun setupSwipeRefresh() {
        binding.swipeRefresh.setOnRefreshListener {
            loadAuthors()
            loadVideos(reset = true)
        }
    }

    private fun loadAuthors() {
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                val response = api.getSubscribedAuthors()

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    authorsAdapter.setAuthors(response.authors)
                    if (response.authors.isNotEmpty()) {
                        binding.rvAuthors.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                        binding.llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
                    } else {
                        binding.rvAuthors.visibility = View.GONE
                        showEmptyState()
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                Toast.makeText(context, "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun loadVideos(reset: Boolean) {
        if (isLoading) return
        if (reset) {
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
        }

        isLoading = true

        loadJob?.cancel()
        loadJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                val response = api.getSubscriptionsFeed(
                    authorId = selectedAuthorId,
                    page = currentPage,
                    perPage = perPage
                )

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    if (reset) {
                        videosAdapter.setVideos(response.videos)
                        binding.rvVideos.scrollToPosition(0)
                    } else {
                        videosAdapter.appendVideos(response.videos)
                    }
                    hasMore = response.hasMore

                    if (response.videos.isEmpty() && reset) {
                        binding.llEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                        binding.swipeRefresh.visibility = View.GONE
                    } else {
                        binding.swipeRefresh.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                        binding.llEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                Toast.makeText(context, "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            } finally {
                isLoading = false
                _binding?.swipeRefresh?.isRefreshing = false
            }
        }
    }

    private fun markAuthorAsViewed(authorId: Int) {
        authorsAdapter.markAuthorAsViewed(authorId)

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                api.markAuthorViewed(authorId)
            } catch (_: Exception) { }
        }
    }

    private fun showEmptyState() {
        _binding?.apply {
            rvAuthors.visibility = View.GONE
            swipeRefresh.visibility = View.GONE
            llEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/SearchAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DiffUtil
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemVideoSearchBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemVideoSkeletonSearchBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.formatDuration
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.timeAgo
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

class SearchAdapter(
    private var videos: List<Video> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (Video) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<RecyclerView.ViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private const val TYPE_VIDEO = 0
        private const val TYPE_SKELETON = 1
        private const val SKELETON_COUNT = 6

        // Общий SimpleDateFormat (с UTC)
        private val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).apply {
            timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        }
    }

    private var showSkeleton = false

    fun setVideos(newVideos: List<Video>) {
        val wasSkeleton = showSkeleton
        showSkeleton = false

        if (wasSkeleton) {
            videos = newVideos
            notifyDataSetChanged()
            return
        }

        val old = videos
        val diff = DiffUtil.calculateDiff(object : DiffUtil.Callback() {
            override fun getOldListSize() = old.size
            override fun getNewListSize() = newVideos.size
            override fun areItemsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o].id == newVideos[n].id
            override fun areContentsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o] == newVideos[n]
        })
        videos = newVideos
        diff.dispatchUpdatesTo(this)
    }

    fun appendVideos(newVideos: List<Video>) {
        if (newVideos.isEmpty()) return
        val startPos = videos.size
        videos = videos + newVideos
        notifyItemRangeInserted(startPos, newVideos.size)
    }

    fun showSkeletons() {
        showSkeleton = true
        videos = emptyList()
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    fun clear() {
        showSkeleton = false
        videos = emptyList()
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun getItemViewType(position: Int): Int =
        if (showSkeleton) TYPE_SKELETON else TYPE_VIDEO

    override fun getItemCount(): Int =
        if (showSkeleton) SKELETON_COUNT else videos.size

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        val inflater = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
        return when (viewType) {
            TYPE_SKELETON -> SkeletonViewHolder(
                ItemVideoSkeletonSearchBinding.inflate(inflater, parent, false)
            )
            else -> VideoViewHolder(
                ItemVideoSearchBinding.inflate(inflater, parent, false)
            )
        }
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: RecyclerView.ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        when (holder) {
            is VideoViewHolder -> holder.bind(videos[position])
            is SkeletonViewHolder -> holder.bind()
        }
    }

    private fun formatViewsShort(n: Int): String = when {
        n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fM", n / 1_000_000.0)
        n >= 1_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fK", n / 1_000.0)
        else -> n.toString()
    }

    private fun timeAgoShort(dateStr: String): String {
        if (dateStr.isEmpty()) return ""
        return try {
            val date = synchronized(dateFmt) { dateFmt.parse(dateStr) } ?: return ""
            val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
            when {
                diff < 60 -> "сейчас"
                diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60} мин"
                diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600} ч"
                diff < 2_592_000 -> "${diff / 86400} дн"
                diff < 31_536_000 -> "${diff / 2_592_000} мес"
                else -> "${diff / 31_536_000} г"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            ""
        }
    }

    inner class VideoViewHolder(private val binding: ItemVideoSearchBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(video: Video) {
            binding.title.text = video.title
            binding.authorName.text = video.author.name

            // Отдельно просмотры и дата — без слова "просмотров" и без точки
            binding.tvViews.text = formatViewsShort(video.views)
            binding.tvDate.text = timeAgoShort(video.published)

            if (video.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.thumbnail.load(video.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            if (video.author.avatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.authorAvatar.load(video.author.avatar) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            if (video.duration > 0) {
                binding.duration.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.duration.text = formatDuration(video.duration)
            } else {
                binding.duration.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(video) }
        }
    }

    class SkeletonViewHolder(private val binding: ItemVideoSkeletonSearchBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {
        fun bind() { /* ничего */ }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/ui/FeedFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.ui

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.viewpager2.adapter.FragmentStateAdapter
import androidx.viewpager2.widget.ViewPager2
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentFeedBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.download.DownloadStorage
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.NetworkUtils

class FeedFragment : Fragment() {

    private var _binding: FragmentFeedBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    // Динамический список табов: "recommended" | "latest" | "downloads"
    private val tabs = mutableListOf<String>()

    // Индекс таба «Скачано» (-1 если его нет)
    private var downloadsTabIndex = -1

    // ===== Слушатель изменений в SharedPreferences («asmr1_downloads») =====
    private var prefsListener: SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentFeedBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        // ===== СОБИРАЕМ СПИСОК ТАБОВ =====
        buildTabsList()

        // ===== НАСТРАИВАЕМ ViewPager2 =====
        setupViewPager()

        // ===== КЛИКИ ПО ТАБАМ =====
        binding.tabRecommended.setOnClickListener { scrollToTab("recommended") }
        binding.tabLatest.setOnClickListener { scrollToTab("latest") }
        binding.tabDownloads.setOnClickListener { scrollToTab("downloads") }

        // ===== УПРАВЛЯЕМ ВИДИМОСТЬЮ ТАБА «СКАЧАНО» =====
        updateDownloadsTabVisibility()

        // ===== СТАРТОВАЯ СТРАНИЦА =====
        val initialIndex = if (downloadsTabIndex >= 0 &&
            !NetworkUtils.isOnline(requireContext())
        ) {
            // Офлайн → сразу на «Скачано»
            downloadsTabIndex
        } else {
            // Онлайн → на первый таб (В тренде)
            0
        }

        binding.viewPager.setCurrentItem(initialIndex, false)
        applyTabStates(initialIndex.toFloat())

        // ===== СЛУШАЕМ ИЗМЕНЕНИЯ =====
        subscribeToDownloadChanges()
    }

    /**
     * Настраивает ViewPager2 с адаптером на основе текущего списка tabs.
     * Вызывается и при первом старте, и при пересборке адаптера.
     */
    private fun setupViewPager() {
        binding.viewPager.offscreenPageLimit = 1
        binding.viewPager.adapter = object : FragmentStateAdapter(this) {
            override fun getItemCount(): Int = tabs.size
            override fun createFragment(position: Int): Fragment {
                return when (tabs.getOrNull(position)) {
                    "downloads" -> DownloadsFragment()
                    "latest" -> VideoListFragment.newInstance("latest")
                    else -> VideoListFragment.newInstance("recommended")
                }
            }
        }

        binding.viewPager.registerOnPageChangeCallback(object : ViewPager2.OnPageChangeCallback() {
            override fun onPageScrolled(
                position: Int,
                positionOffset: Float,
                positionOffsetPixels: Int
            ) {
                applyTabStates(position + positionOffset)
            }

            override fun onPageSelected(position: Int) {
                for (i in tabs.indices) {
                    if (i != position) {
                        scrollToTopAt(i)
                    }
                }
            }
        })
    }

    /**
     * Подписка на изменения в prefs "asmr1_downloads".
     * Когда что-то скачано/удалено — пересобираем табы.
     */
    private fun subscribeToDownloadChanges() {
        val prefs = requireContext()
            .getSharedPreferences("asmr1_downloads", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

        prefsListener = SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener { _, key ->
            if (key == "downloads_index") {
                // Что-то изменилось в списке загрузок
                refreshTabsIfNeeded()
            }
        }

        prefs.registerOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(prefsListener)
    }

    /**
     * Пересобирает список табов. Если состав изменился —
     * пересоздаёт адаптер, чтобы ViewPager2 подхватил новый таб.
     */
    private fun refreshTabsIfNeeded() {
        if (_binding == null) return

        val oldIndex = downloadsTabIndex
        val oldCount = tabs.size

        buildTabsList()
        updateDownloadsTabVisibility()

        if (tabs.size != oldCount || downloadsTabIndex != oldIndex) {
            // Количество табов изменилось — пересоздаём адаптер
            setupViewPager()
            // Возвращаемся на первый таб (или сохраняем текущую позицию если возможно)
            val newIndex = if (downloadsTabIndex >= 0 &&
                !NetworkUtils.isOnline(requireContext())
            ) {
                downloadsTabIndex
            } else {
                0
            }
            binding.viewPager.setCurrentItem(newIndex, false)
            applyTabStates(newIndex.toFloat())
        }
    }

    /**
     * Собирает список табов на основе состояния.
     */
    private fun buildTabsList() {
        tabs.clear()
        downloadsTabIndex = -1

        val storage = DownloadStorage(requireContext())
        val hasDownloads = storage.hasAnyDownloads()
        val isOnline = NetworkUtils.isOnline(requireContext())

        tabs.add("recommended")
        tabs.add("latest")

        if (hasDownloads || !isOnline) {
            tabs.add("downloads")
            downloadsTabIndex = 2
        }
    }

    private fun updateDownloadsTabVisibility() {
        binding.tabDownloads.visibility = if (downloadsTabIndex >= 0) {
            View.VISIBLE
        } else {
            View.GONE
        }
    }

    private fun scrollToTab(name: String) {
        val index = tabs.indexOf(name)
        if (index < 0) return
        if (binding.viewPager.currentItem != index) {
            binding.viewPager.setCurrentItem(index, true)
        }
    }

    private fun scrollToTopAt(index: Int) {
        val tag = "f$index"
        val frag = childFragmentManager.findFragmentByTag(tag)
        if (frag is VideoListFragment) {
            frag.scrollToTop()
        }
    }

    private fun applyTabStates(progress: Float) {
        for (i in tabs.indices) {
            val dist = kotlin.math.abs(progress - i)
            val weight = (1f - dist).coerceIn(0f, 1f)

            val tabView = when (tabs[i]) {
                "recommended" -> binding.tabRecommended
                "latest" -> binding.tabLatest
                "downloads" -> binding.tabDownloads
                else -> null
            }
            tabView?.let { applyTabStateToView(it, weight) }
        }
    }

    private fun applyTabStateToView(tab: View, weight: Float) {
        val scale = 0.94f + 0.06f * weight
        tab.scaleX = scale
        tab.scaleY = scale
        tab.alpha = 0.6f + 0.4f * weight
        tab.background?.mutate()?.alpha = (weight * 255).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        // На всякий случай — проверяем и здесь (если что-то поменялось через другие пути)
        refreshTabsIfNeeded()
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()

        // Отписываемся от prefs
        if (prefsListener != null) {
            val prefs = requireContext()
