package ru.asmr1.app.author

import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.fragment.app.FragmentActivity
import androidx.viewpager2.adapter.FragmentStateAdapter
import ru.asmr1.app.data.AuthorPageResponse

class AuthorPagerAdapter(
    activity: FragmentActivity,
    private val data: AuthorPageResponse
) : FragmentStateAdapter(activity) {

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = 4

    override fun createFragment(position: Int): Fragment {
        return when (position) {
            0 -> AuthorHomeFragment.newInstance(data)
            1 -> AuthorVideosFragment.newInstance(data)
            2 -> AuthorPlaylistsFragment.newInstance(data)
            3 -> AuthorAboutFragment.newInstance(data)
            else -> Fragment()
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/author/AuthorPageFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.author

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.transition.AutoTransition
import androidx.transition.TransitionManager
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import coil.load
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.AuthorPageResponse
import ru.asmr1.app.data.SubscribeRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentAuthorPageBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.formatViewsShort

class AuthorPageFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        const val ARG_AUTHOR_ID = "AUTHOR_ID"
        fun newInstance(authorId: Int) = AuthorPageFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putInt(ARG_AUTHOR_ID, authorId) }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentAuthorPageBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private var authorId: Int = -1
    private var authorData: AuthorPageResponse? = null

    private var subscriptionState: Int = 0
    private var subscribersCount: Int = 0
    private var subscribeInFlight: Boolean = false

    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentAuthorPageBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        // Жёстко скрываем чипы при старте — они появятся только на вкладке "Видео"

        authorId = arguments?.getInt(ARG_AUTHOR_ID, -1) ?: -1
        if (authorId <= 0) {
            (activity as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity)?.closeAuthorPage()
            return
        }

        binding.btnClose.setOnClickListener {
            (activity as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity)?.closeAuthorPage()
        }

        // Клик по описанию → вкладка "Об авторе"
        binding.tvDescription.setOnClickListener {
            binding.viewPager.setCurrentItem(3, true)
        }

        setupSwipeToClose()

        binding.btnSubscribe.setOnClickListener {
            if (subscriptionState == 0) performSubscribe("sub")
            else performSubscribe("unsub")
        }
        binding.btnBell.setOnClickListener { showSubscriptionMenu() }

        // Клик по аватару → модалка с увеличенным аватаром
        binding.ivAvatar.setOnClickListener {
            val url = authorData?.avatar ?: return@setOnClickListener
            showAvatarPreview(url)
        }

        loadAuthor()
    }

    private fun loadAuthor() {
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val response = ApiClient.service.getUserPage(authorId)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                authorData = response
                bindAuthor(response)
                loadSubscriptionStatus()
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка загрузки: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                (activity as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity)?.closeAuthorPage()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun bindAuthor(data: AuthorPageResponse) {
        val uname = "@" + data.nickname.lowercase().replace(" ", "_").replace("[^a-z0-9_]".toRegex(), "")

        binding.tvNickname.text = data.nickname
        binding.tvUsername.text = uname
        binding.tvSubscribers.text = formatViewsShort(data.subscribersCount)
        binding.tvVideos.text = data.videoCount.toString()
        binding.tvViews.text = formatViewsShort(data.totalViews)

        if (data.avatar.isNotEmpty()) {
            binding.ivAvatar.load(data.avatar) {
                crossfade(true)
                placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                error(R.color.card_bg)
            }
        }

        if (!data.description.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            binding.tvDescription.text = data.description
        } else {
            binding.tvDescription.visibility = View.GONE
        }

        subscriptionState = if (data.followStatus) 1 else 0
        subscribersCount = data.subscribersCount
        updateSubscriptionUi()

        setupPager(data)
        setupAuthorVideoChips()
    }


    // ============================================================
    // ЧИПЫ СОРТИРОВКИ ВИДЕО (в activity_main.xml, внизу над меню)
    // ============================================================

    private fun setupAuthorVideoChips() {
        val chips = activity?.findViewById<android.view.View>(R.id.author_video_chips) ?: return
        val chipNew = activity?.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.author_chip_new)
        val chipOld = activity?.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.author_chip_old)
        val chipViews = activity?.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.author_chip_views)

        fun refresh(mode: String) {
            fun setChip(chip: android.widget.TextView?, active: Boolean) {
                chip?.isSelected = active
                chip?.setTextColor(
                    androidx.core.content.ContextCompat.getColor(
                        requireContext(),
                        if (active) android.R.color.white else R.color.text_secondary
                    )
                )
            }
            setChip(chipNew, mode == "new")
            setChip(chipOld, mode == "old")
            setChip(chipViews, mode == "views")
        }

        fun getVideosFragment(): AuthorVideosFragment? {
            // ViewPager2 не даёт теги f0/f1 — ищем по типу рекурсивно
            fun searchRecursive(fm: androidx.fragment.app.FragmentManager): AuthorVideosFragment? {
                for (f in fm.fragments) {
                    if (f is AuthorVideosFragment) return f
                    val found = searchRecursive(f.childFragmentManager)
                    if (found != null) return found
                }
                return null
            }
            return searchRecursive(childFragmentManager)
        }

        fun setChipsVisible(visible: Boolean) {
            chips.animate().cancel()
            chips.animate().alpha(if (visible) 1f else 0f).setDuration(150).start()
        }

        chipNew?.setOnClickListener { getVideosFragment()?.setSort("new"); refresh("new") }
        chipOld?.setOnClickListener { getVideosFragment()?.setSort("old"); refresh("old") }
        chipViews?.setOnClickListener { getVideosFragment()?.setSort("views"); refresh("views") }

        binding.viewPager.registerOnPageChangeCallback(object : androidx.viewpager2.widget.ViewPager2.OnPageChangeCallback() {
            override fun onPageSelected(position: Int) {
                setChipsVisible(position == 1)
                if (position == 1) refresh(getVideosFragment()?.getSortMode() ?: "new")
            }
        })

        setChipsVisible(binding.viewPager.currentItem == 1)
        refresh("new")
    }

    private fun setupPager(data: AuthorPageResponse) {
        val adapter = AuthorPagerAdapter(requireActivity(), data)
        binding.viewPager.adapter = adapter
        // Предзагрузка всех 4 вкладок — убирает лаг при первом переключении
        binding.viewPager.offscreenPageLimit = 4

        com.google.android.material.tabs.TabLayoutMediator(
            binding.tabLayout,
            binding.viewPager
        ) { tab, position ->
            tab.text = when (position) {
                0 -> "Главная"
                1 -> "Видео"
                2 -> "Плейлисты"
                3 -> "Об авторе"
                else -> ""
            }
        }.attach()
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ПОДПИСКА
    // ============================================================

    private fun loadSubscriptionStatus() {
        if (authorId <= 0) return
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).getSubscriptionStatus(authorId)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                if (response.success) {
                    subscribersCount = response.subscribersCount
                    subscriptionState = when {
                        !response.subscribed -> 0
                        response.notificationsEnabled -> 1
                        else -> 2
                    }
                    updateSubscriptionUi()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) { }
        }
    }

    private fun performSubscribe(action: String) {
        if (subscribeInFlight) return
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите чтобы подписаться", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }
        if (authorId <= 0) return

        subscribeInFlight = true
        val oldState = subscriptionState
        val oldCount = subscribersCount

        when (action) {
            "sub"    -> { subscriptionState = 1; subscribersCount = oldCount + 1 }
            "unsub"  -> { subscriptionState = 0; subscribersCount = (oldCount - 1).coerceAtLeast(0) }
            "notify" -> subscriptionState = 1
            "mute"   -> subscriptionState = 2
        }
        updateSubscriptionUi()

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                    .subscribe(authorId, SubscribeRequest(action))
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                if (response.success) {
                    subscribersCount = response.subscribersCount
                    subscriptionState = when {
                        !response.subscribed -> 0
                        response.notificationsEnabled -> 1
                        else -> 2
                    }
                    updateSubscriptionUi()
                } else {
                    subscriptionState = oldState
                    subscribersCount = oldCount
                    updateSubscriptionUi()
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                subscriptionState = oldState
                subscribersCount = oldCount
                updateSubscriptionUi()
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка сети", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            } finally {
                subscribeInFlight = false
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateSubscriptionUi() {
        if (_binding == null) return
        val subscribed = subscriptionState != 0
        val iconRes = if (subscriptionState == 1) R.drawable.ic_nav_bell else R.drawable.ic_bell_off

        if (!subscribed) {
            binding.btnSubscribe.visibility = android.view.View.VISIBLE
            binding.btnSubscribe.text = "Подписаться"
            binding.btnBell.visibility = android.view.View.GONE
        } else {
            binding.btnSubscribe.visibility = android.view.View.GONE
            binding.btnBell.visibility = android.view.View.VISIBLE
            binding.bellIcon.setImageResource(iconRes)
        }
    }

    private fun showSubscriptionMenu() {
        try {
            val sheetView = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.sheet_subscription_menu, null)
            val dialog = com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialog(requireContext())
            dialog.setContentView(sheetView)
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.subs_menu_notify)?.setOnClickListener {
                performSubscribe("notify"); dialog.dismiss()
            }
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.subs_menu_mute)?.setOnClickListener {
                performSubscribe("mute"); dialog.dismiss()
            }
            sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.subs_menu_unsubscribe)?.setOnClickListener {
                performSubscribe("unsub"); dialog.dismiss()
            }
            dialog.show()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка меню", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }


    // ============================================================
    // СВАЙП ВНИЗ ПО ШАПКЕ = ЗАКРЫТИЕ СТРАНИЦЫ АВТОРА
    // ============================================================

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    private fun setupSwipeToClose() {
        val activity = activity as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity ?: return
        val container = activity.findViewById<android.view.View>(R.id.author_container) ?: return
        val appBar = binding.headerBlock

        var startY = 0f
        var isDragging = false
        val thresholdPx = 150 * resources.displayMetrics.density

        appBar.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            when (event.actionMasked) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    startY = event.rawY
                    isDragging = false
                    false
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - startY
                    if (dy > 20f) {
                        isDragging = true
                        // Только вниз, не вверх
                        container.translationY = dy.coerceAtLeast(0f)
                        true
                    } else {
                        false
                    }
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - startY
                    if (isDragging && dy > thresholdPx) {
                        // Закрываем
                        container.translationY = 0f
                        activity.closeAuthorPage()
                    } else {
                        // Возвращаем
                        container.animate()
                            .translationY(0f)
                            .setDuration(180)
                            .start()
                    }
                    isDragging = false
                    true
                }
                else -> false
            }
        }
    }





    // ============================================================
    // ПРОКИДКА СКРОЛЛА В AppBarLayout
    // ============================================================

    fun scrollAppBar(dy: Int) {
        if (!isAdded) return
        if (_binding == null) return
        binding.appBar.setExpanded(dy <= 0, true)
    }


    // ============================================================
    // МОДАЛКА С УВЕЛИЧЕННЫМ АВАТАРОМ
    // ============================================================

    private fun showAvatarPreview(url: String) {
        val dialog = android.app.Dialog(requireContext(), android.R.style.Theme_Translucent_NoTitleBar_Fullscreen)
        val view = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.dialog_avatar_preview, null)
        dialog.setContentView(view)

        val img = view.findViewById<com.google.android.material.imageview.ShapeableImageView>(R.id.avatar_preview_image)
        val closeBtn = view.findViewById<android.widget.ImageButton>(R.id.avatar_preview_close)
        val root = view.findViewById<android.view.View>(R.id.avatar_preview_root)

        img.load(url) {
            crossfade(true)
            placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
            error(R.color.card_bg)
        }

        closeBtn.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
        root.setOnClickListener { dialog.dismiss() }
        img.setOnClickListener { /* не закрываем при клике на аватар */ }

        dialog.setOnShowListener {
            val window = dialog.window
            if (window != null) {
                window.setLayout(
                    android.view.ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                    android.view.ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
                )
                window.setBackgroundDrawableResource(android.R.color.transparent)
                window.setDimAmount(0f)
            }
        }

        dialog.show()
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        activity?.findViewById<android.view.View>(R.id.author_video_chips)
            ?.animate()?.alpha(0f)?.setDuration(120)?.start()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/author/AuthorVideosFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.author

import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.AuthorPageResponse
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentAuthorVideosBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.player.AddToPlaylistSheet
import ru.asmr1.app.ui.VideoAdapter

class AuthorVideosFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_DATA = "data"
        fun newInstance(data: AuthorPageResponse) = AuthorVideosFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putParcelable(ARG_DATA, data) }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentAuthorVideosBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: VideoAdapter
    private var allVideos: List<Video> = emptyList()
    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager

    private var sortMode: String = "new"

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater, container: ViewGroup?, savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentAuthorVideosBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        val data = arguments?.getParcelable<AuthorPageResponse>(ARG_DATA) ?: return
        allVideos = data.tabs?.videos ?: emptyList()

        adapter = VideoAdapter(
            videos = emptyList(),
            onClick = { video ->
                (requireActivity() as? ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity)?.openPlayer(video)
            },
            onMoreClick = { video, _ -> showVideoMenu(video) },
            hideAuthor = true
        )

        binding.recyclerView.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.recyclerView.adapter = adapter

        binding.recyclerView.addOnScrollListener(object : androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(rv: androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                val header = parentFragment as? AuthorPageFragment ?: return
                header.scrollAppBar(dy)
            }
        })
        binding.swipeRefresh.isEnabled = false

        applySort()
    }

    fun setSort(mode: String) {
        if (sortMode == mode) return
        sortMode = mode
        applySort()
        binding.recyclerView.scrollToPosition(0)
    }

    fun getSortMode(): String = sortMode

    private fun applySort() {
        android.util.Log.e("AuthorSort", "sortMode=$sortMode, allVideos=$allVideos")
        allVideos.take(3).forEach {
            android.util.Log.e("AuthorSort", "video id=${it.id}, published='${it.published}', views=${it.views}")
        }
        val sorted = when (sortMode) {
            "old" -> allVideos.sortedBy { it.published }
            "views" -> allVideos.sortedByDescending { it.views }
            else -> allVideos.sortedByDescending { it.published }
        }
        android.util.Log.e("AuthorSort", "sorted first 3: ${sorted.take(3).map { it.id }}")
        adapter.setVideos(sorted)
    }

    // ============================================================
    // МЕНЮ ПОД КАРТОЧКОЙ (⋯)
    // ============================================================

    private fun showVideoMenu(video: Video) {
        val sheetView = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.sheet_author_video_menu, null)
        val dialog = com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialog(requireContext())
        dialog.setContentView(sheetView)

        // 1. Смотреть позже
        sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_watch_later)?.setOnClickListener {
            dialog.dismiss()
            addToWatchLater(video)
        }

        // 2. Сохранить в плейлист
        sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_save_playlist)?.setOnClickListener {
            dialog.dismiss()
            if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы сохранять", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                return@setOnClickListener
            }
            val sheet = AddToPlaylistSheet.newInstance(video.id)
            sheet.show(childFragmentManager, "add_to_playlist")
        }

        // 3. Поделиться
        sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_share)?.setOnClickListener {
            dialog.dismiss()
            shareVideo(video)
        }

        // 4. Скачать видео
        sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_download)?.setOnClickListener {
            dialog.dismiss()
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Скачивание — скоро", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        // 5. Отмена
        sheetView.findViewById<View>(R.id.menu_cancel)?.setOnClickListener {
            dialog.dismiss()
        }

        dialog.show()
    }

    // ============================================================
    // СМОТРЕТЬ ПОЗЖЕ
    // ============================================================

    private fun addToWatchLater(video: Video) {
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы сохранять", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)

                // Проверяем, есть ли уже в "Смотреть позже"
                val profile = api.getMeProfile()
                val watchLaterId = profile.watchlater.playlistId
                if (watchLaterId <= 0) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка плейлиста", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    return@launch
                }

                val response = api.addToPlaylist(watchLaterId, video.id)
                if (response.success) {
                    val msg = if (response.alreadyIn) "Уже в «Смотреть позже»" else "Добавлено в «Смотреть позже»"
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), msg, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ПОДЕЛИТЬСЯ
    // ============================================================

    private fun shareVideo(video: Video) {
        try {
            val url = "https://asmr1.ru/watch/${video.id}/"
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                type = "text/plain"
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, url + "\n\n" + video.title)
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, video.title)
            }
            startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Поделиться"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/author/AuthorAboutFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.author

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import ru.asmr1.app.data.AuthorPageResponse
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentAuthorAboutBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.timeAgo

class AuthorAboutFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_DATA = "data"
        fun newInstance(data: AuthorPageResponse) = AuthorAboutFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putParcelable(ARG_DATA, data) }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentAuthorAboutBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater, container: ViewGroup?, savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentAuthorAboutBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        val data = arguments?.getParcelable<AuthorPageResponse>(ARG_DATA) ?: return

        binding.tvNickname.text = data.nickname
        binding.tvDescription.text = data.description ?: "Описание не заполнено"
        binding.tvSubscribers.text = data.subscribersCount.toString()
        binding.tvVideos.text = data.videoCount.toString()
        binding.tvViews.text = data.totalViews.toString()

        if (!data.channelJoined.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            binding.tvJoined.text = "На сайте с ${data.channelJoined.take(10)}"
        } else {
            binding.tvJoined.visibility = View.GONE
        }

        // Соцсети
        val s = data.socials
        binding.socialsContainer.removeAllViews()
        if (s != null) {
            val map = mapOf(
                "YouTube" to s.youtube,
                "Telegram" to s.telegram,
                "VK" to s.vk,
                "Rutube" to s.rutube,
                "Дзен" to s.dzen,
                "Boosty" to s.boosty,
                "Patreon" to s.patreon,
                "DonationAlerts" to s.donationalerts,
                "Сайт" to s.website
            )
            for ((name, url) in map) {
                if (!url.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                    val tv = android.widget.TextView(requireContext())
                    tv.text = "$name: $url"
                    tv.setTextColor(0xFFE6E6E6.toInt())
                    tv.textSize = 14f
                    tv.setPadding(0, 8, 0, 8)
                    binding.socialsContainer.addView(tv)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/author/AuthorHomeFragment.kt =====

class AuthorHomeFragment : Fragment() {

    // ============================================================

    private fun showVideoMenu(video: Video) {

            dialog.dismiss()
        }

        dialog.show()
    }

    private fun addToWatchLater(video: Video) {
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы сохранять", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                val profile = api.getMeProfile()
                val watchLaterId = profile.watchlater.playlistId
                if (watchLaterId <= 0) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка плейлиста", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    return@launch
                }
                val response = api.addToPlaylist(watchLaterId, video.id)
                if (response.success) {
                    val msg = if (response.alreadyIn) "Уже в «Смотреть позже»" else "Добавлено в «Смотреть позже»"
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), msg, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun shareVideo(video: Video) {
        try {
            val url = "https://asmr1.ru/watch/${video.id}/"
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                type = "text/plain"
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, url + "\n\n" + video.title)
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, video.title)
            }
            startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Поделиться"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/utils/SearchHistoryManager.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.utils

import android.content.Context

/**
 * Локальная история поиска — сохраняется в SharedPreferences.
 * Максимум 20 запросов, новые сверху, без дубликатов.
 */
class SearchHistoryManager(context: Context) {

    private val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("asmr1_search_history", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    companion object {
        private const val KEY_HISTORY = "history_items"
        private const val SEPARATOR = "|||"
        private const val MAX_ITEMS = 20
    }

    fun getHistory(): List<String> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_HISTORY, null) ?: return emptyList()
        if (raw.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
        return raw.split(SEPARATOR).filter { it.isNotBlank() }
    }

    fun addQuery(query: String) {
        val trimmed = query.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return

        val current = getHistory().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.equals(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
        current.add(0, trimmed)
        val limited = current.take(MAX_ITEMS)
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_HISTORY, limited.joinToString(SEPARATOR)).apply()
    }

    fun removeQuery(query: String) {
        val current = getHistory().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it == query }
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_HISTORY, current.joinToString(SEPARATOR)).apply()
    }

    fun clearAll() {
        prefs.edit().remove(KEY_HISTORY).apply()
    }

    fun isEmpty(): Boolean = getHistory().isEmpty()
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/utils/Formatters.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.utils

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

fun formatViews(n: Int): String = when {
    n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format("%.1fM просмотров", n / 1_000_000.0)
    n >= 1_000 -> String.format("%.1fK просмотров", n / 1_000.0)
    else -> "$n просмотров"
}

fun formatDuration(sec: Int): String {
    val m = sec / 60
    val s = sec % 60
    return "$m:${if (s < 10) "0$s" else s}"
}

fun timeAgo(dateStr: String): String {
    if (dateStr.isEmpty()) return ""
    return try {
        val fmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).apply {
            timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        }
        val date = fmt.parse(dateStr) ?: return ""
        val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
        when {
            diff < 60 -> "только что"
            diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60} мин назад"
            diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600} ч назад"
            diff < 2592000 -> "${diff / 86400} дн назад"
            diff < 31536000 -> "${diff / 2592000} мес назад"
            else -> "${diff / 31536000} г назад"
        }
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        ""
    }
}


/**
 * Короткий формат БЕЗ слова "просмотров" — для мест, где рядом уже есть подпись.
 * Пример: 1500 -> "1.5K", 1500000 -> "1.5M", 42 -> "42"
 */
fun formatViewsShort(n: Int): String = when {
    n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1fM", n / 1_000_000.0)
    n >= 1_000 -> String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1fK", n / 1_000.0)
    else -> n.toString()
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/utils/NetworkUtils.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.utils

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.os.Build

object NetworkUtils {

    /**
     * Проверяет, есть ли доступ к интернету.
     * Использует ConnectivityManager — работает мгновенно, не требует запросов.
     */
    fun isOnline(context: Context): Boolean {
        val cm = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ConnectivityManager
            ?: return false

        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            val network = cm.activeNetwork ?: return false
            val caps = cm.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return false
            caps.hasCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET) &&
                    caps.hasCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_VALIDATED)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            cm.activeNetworkInfo?.isConnectedOrConnecting == true
        }
    }
}

package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.view.LayoutInflater

/**
 * Универсальная карточка плейлиста:
 *  - watchlater / liked / custom
 */
data class PlaylistCard(
    val id: Int,
    val title: String,
    val count: Int,
    val type: String, // "watchlater" | "liked" | "custom"
    val firstVideo: ProfileVideo? = null,
    val visibility: String? = null // "public" | "private" (для custom)
)

class PlaylistBigAdapter(
    private var items: List<PlaylistCard> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (PlaylistCard) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<PlaylistBigAdapter.PlaylistViewHolder>() {

    fun setItems(newItems: List<PlaylistCard>) {
        items = newItems
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): PlaylistViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemPlaylistBigBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return PlaylistViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: PlaylistViewHolder, position: Int) {
        holder.bind(items[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class PlaylistViewHolder(private val binding: ItemPlaylistBigBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(card: PlaylistCard) {
            binding.plTitle.text = card.title
            binding.plCount.text = "${card.count} видео"

            val first = card.firstVideo
            if (first != null && first.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.plThumb.load(first.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                }
            } else {
                binding.plThumb.setImageResource(R.color.card_bg)
            }

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(card) }
        }
    }
}
