package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DiffUtil
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ProfileVideo
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemHistoryVideoBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.formatDuration
import java.util.Locale

class HistoryAdapter(
    private var items: List<ProfileVideo> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (ProfileVideo) -> Unit = {},
    private val onRemove: (ProfileVideo) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<HistoryAdapter.VH>() {

    fun setItems(newItems: List<ProfileVideo>) {
        val old = items
        val diff = DiffUtil.calculateDiff(object : DiffUtil.Callback() {
            override fun getOldListSize() = old.size
            override fun getNewListSize() = newItems.size
            override fun areItemsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o].id == newItems[n].id
            override fun areContentsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o] == newItems[n]
        })
        items = newItems
        diff.dispatchUpdatesTo(this)
    }

    fun getItems(): List<ProfileVideo> = items

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): VH {
        val binding = ItemHistoryVideoBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return VH(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: VH, position: Int) = holder.bind(items[position])

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class VH(private val binding: ItemHistoryVideoBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(video: ProfileVideo) {
            binding.hvTitle.text = video.title
            binding.hvAuthor.text = video.authorName
            binding.hvMeta.text = formatViewsShort(video.views)

            if (video.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.hvThumb.load(video.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                }
            }

            if (video.duration > 0) {
                binding.hvDuration.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.hvDuration.text = formatDuration(video.duration)
            } else {
                binding.hvDuration.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            // Прогресс пока не приходит — скрываем. Позже можно добавить.
            binding.hvProgress.visibility = View.GONE

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(video) }
            binding.hvRemove.setOnClickListener { onRemove(video) }
        }
    }

    private fun formatViewsShort(n: Int): String = when {
        n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fM", n / 1_000_000.0)
        n >= 1_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fK", n / 1_000.0)
        else -> "$n"
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/profile/PlaylistVideoAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.util.Log
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DefaultItemAnimator
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ProfileVideo
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemPlaylistVideoBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.formatDuration
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.abs

class PlaylistVideoAdapter(
    private var items: MutableList<ProfileVideo> = mutableListOf(),
    private val canEdit: Boolean,
    private val onClick: (ProfileVideo) -> Unit = {},
    private val onDeleteSwipe: (ProfileVideo, View) -> Unit = { _, _ -> },
    private val onReorder: (List<ProfileVideo>) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<PlaylistVideoAdapter.ViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private const val DELETE_THRESHOLD_RATIO = 0.35f
        private const val MAX_SWIPE_DP = 300f
        private const val SWIPE_START_DP = 10f
    }

    /** Ссылка на ItemTouchHelper, чтобы стартовать drag прямо из ViewHolder */
    private var touchHelper: ItemTouchHelper? = null

    fun setItems(newItems: List<ProfileVideo>) {
        items = newItems.toMutableList()
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    fun getItems(): List<ProfileVideo> = items

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): ViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemPlaylistVideoBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return ViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        if (position in items.indices) holder.bind(items[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class ViewHolder(val binding: ItemPlaylistVideoBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        private val density = binding.root.resources.displayMetrics.density
        private val maxSwipePx = MAX_SWIPE_DP * density
        private val swipeStartPx = SWIPE_START_DP * density

        init {
            if (canEdit) setupTouch()
        }

        fun bind(video: ProfileVideo) {
            binding.plvTitle.text = video.title
            binding.plvAuthor.text = video.authorName
            binding.plvMeta.text = formatViewsShort(video.views)

            if (video.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.plvThumb.load(video.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                }
            }

            if (video.duration > 0) {
                binding.plvDuration.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.plvDuration.text = formatDuration(video.duration)
            } else {
                binding.plvDuration.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            binding.dragHandle.visibility = if (canEdit) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

            binding.plVideoCard.translationX = 0f
            binding.plVideoCard.alpha = 1f
            binding.plVideoCard.elevation = 0f
            binding.root.alpha = 1f

            binding.plVideoCard.setOnClickListener { onClick(video) }
        }

        /**
         * Единый обработчик тача на карточке:
         *  - если DOWN попал по drag_handle  → сразу startDrag
         *  - иначе                            → логика свайпа влево
         */
        @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
        private fun setupTouch() {
            val card = binding.plVideoCard
            var startX = 0f
            var startY = 0f
            var isSwiping = false
            var isDragging = false
            var downTime = 0L

            card.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
                when (event.actionMasked) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        startX = event.rawX
                        startY = event.rawY
                        isSwiping = false
                        isDragging = false
                        downTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

                        // ===== Если тап по ручке — сразу стартуем drag =====
                        if (canEdit && isTouchOnHandle(event)) {
                            isDragging = true
                            val helper = touchHelper
                            if (helper != null) {
                                helper.startDrag(this)
                            }
                            return@setOnTouchListener true
                        }
                        false
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        // Если drag — отдаём управление ItemTouchHelper
                        if (isDragging) return@setOnTouchListener true

                        val dx = event.rawX - startX
                        val dy = event.rawY - startY

                        if (!isSwiping) {
                            if (dx < -swipeStartPx && abs(dx) > abs(dy) * 1.5f) {
                                isSwiping = true
                                card.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(true)
                            }
                        }

                        if (isSwiping) {
                            card.translationX = dx.coerceIn(-maxSwipePx, 0f)
                            true
                        } else {
                            false
                        }
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                        if (isDragging) {
                            isDragging = false
                            return@setOnTouchListener true
                        }

                        if (isSwiping) {
                            val dx = card.translationX
                            val threshold = -card.width * DELETE_THRESHOLD_RATIO
                            val position = bindingAdapterPosition
                            val video = if (position in items.indices) items[position] else null

                            if (dx < threshold && video != null) {
                                card.animate()
                                    .translationX(-card.width.toFloat())
                                    .alpha(0f)
                                    .setDuration(180)
                                    .withEndAction {
                                        onDeleteSwipe(video, card)
                                    }
                                    .start()
                            } else {
                                card.animate()
                                    .translationX(0f)
                                    .setDuration(180)
                                    .start()
                            }
                            card.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(false)
                            isSwiping = false
                            true
                        } else {
                            val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - downTime
                            if (elapsed < 400) {
                                val position = bindingAdapterPosition
                                if (position in items.indices) onClick(items[position])
                            }
                            false
                        }
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                        if (isSwiping) {
                            card.animate()
                                .translationX(0f)
                                .setDuration(180)
                                .start()
                            card.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(false)
                            isSwiping = false
                        }
                        isDragging = false
                        false
                    }

                    else -> false
                }
            }
        }

        private fun isTouchOnHandle(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
            val handle = binding.dragHandle
            if (handle.width == 0 || handle.height == 0) return false

            // Координаты тача относительно самой карточки
            val cardLoc = IntArray(2)
            binding.plVideoCard.getLocationOnScreen(cardLoc)
            val localX = event.rawX - cardLoc[0]
            val localY = event.rawY - cardLoc[1]

            // Координаты ручки относительно карточки (тот же родитель → local всегда в одном фрейме)
            val hx = handle.left.toFloat()
            val hy = handle.top.toFloat()
            val hw = handle.width.toFloat()
            val hh = handle.height.toFloat()

            // Запас 8dp, чтобы палец не промахивался
            val padding = 8 * density

            return localX >= hx - padding && localX <= hx + hw + padding &&
                    localY >= hy - padding && localY <= hy + hh + padding
        }
    }

    private fun formatViewsShort(n: Int): String = when {
        n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fM", n / 1_000_000.0)
        n >= 1_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fK", n / 1_000.0)
        else -> "$n"
    }

    fun attachToRecyclerView(recyclerView: RecyclerView) {
        recyclerView.itemAnimator = DefaultItemAnimator().apply {
            removeDuration = 200
            addDuration = 150
            moveDuration = 200
            changeDuration = 150
        }

        if (!canEdit) return

        val callback = object : ItemTouchHelper.SimpleCallback(
            ItemTouchHelper.UP or ItemTouchHelper.DOWN,
            0
        ) {
            override fun getMovementFlags(
                rv: RecyclerView,
                vh: RecyclerView.ViewHolder
            ): Int = makeMovementFlags(
                ItemTouchHelper.UP or ItemTouchHelper.DOWN,
                0
            )

            override fun isLongPressDragEnabled(): Boolean = false
            override fun isItemViewSwipeEnabled(): Boolean = false

            override fun onMove(
                rv: RecyclerView,
                vh: RecyclerView.ViewHolder,
                target: RecyclerView.ViewHolder
            ): Boolean {
                val from = vh.bindingAdapterPosition
                val to = target.bindingAdapterPosition
                if (from == RecyclerView.NO_POSITION || to == RecyclerView.NO_POSITION) return false
                if (from !in items.indices || to !in items.indices) return false

                val moved = items.removeAt(from)
                items.add(to, moved)
                notifyItemMoved(from, to)
                return true
            }

            override fun onSwiped(vh: RecyclerView.ViewHolder, direction: Int) {}

            override fun clearView(rv: RecyclerView, vh: RecyclerView.ViewHolder) {
                super.clearView(rv, vh)
                vh.itemView.alpha = 1f
                vh.itemView.elevation = 0f
                vh.itemView.translationX = 0f
                vh.itemView.translationY = 0f
                vh.itemView.scaleX = 1f
                vh.itemView.scaleY = 1f

                onReorder(items.toList())
            }

            override fun onSelectedChanged(vh: RecyclerView.ViewHolder?, actionState: Int) {
                super.onSelectedChanged(vh, actionState)
                if (actionState == ItemTouchHelper.ACTION_STATE_DRAG && vh != null) {
                    vh.itemView.alpha = 0.9f
                    vh.itemView.elevation = 20f
                    vh.itemView.scaleX = 1.02f
                    vh.itemView.scaleY = 1.02f
                }
            }

            override fun onChildDraw(
                c: Canvas,
                rv: RecyclerView,
                vh: RecyclerView.ViewHolder,
                dX: Float,
                dY: Float,
                actionState: Int,
                isCurrentlyActive: Boolean
            ) {
                super.onChildDraw(c, rv, vh, dX, dY, actionState, isCurrentlyActive)
            }
        }

        val helper = ItemTouchHelper(callback)
        helper.attachToRecyclerView(recyclerView)
        touchHelper = helper
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/profile/HistoryFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Author
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ProfileVideo
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentHistoryBinding

class HistoryFragment : Fragment() {

    private var _binding: FragmentHistoryBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager
    private lateinit var adapter: HistoryAdapter

    private var currentPage = 1
    private var isLoading = false
    private var hasMore = true
    private val perPage = 20

    private var loadJob: Job? = null
    private var removeJob: Job? = null
    private var clearJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentHistoryBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        binding.btnBack.setOnClickListener {
            parentFragmentManager.popBackStack()
        }

        binding.btnClearHistory.setOnClickListener { confirmClearHistory() }

        setupRecycler()
        setupSwipeRefresh()
        loadHistory(reset = true)
    }

    private fun setupRecycler() {
        adapter = HistoryAdapter(
            onClick = { pv -> openPlayer(pv) },
            onRemove = { pv -> removeFromHistory(pv) }
        )
        binding.historyRecycler.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.historyRecycler.adapter = adapter
        binding.historyRecycler.setHasFixedSize(false)

        binding.historyRecycler.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(rv: RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                if (dy <= 0) return
                if (isLoading || !hasMore) return
                val lm = rv.layoutManager as? LinearLayoutManager ?: return
                val lastVisible = lm.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (lastVisible >= adapter.itemCount - 3 && adapter.itemCount > 0) {
                    currentPage++
                    loadHistory(reset = false)
                }
            }
        })
    }

    private fun setupSwipeRefresh() {
        binding.swipeRefresh.setOnRefreshListener {
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
            loadHistory(reset = true)
        }
    }

    private fun loadHistory(reset: Boolean) {
        if (isLoading) return
        if (!hasMore && !reset) return

        if (reset) {
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
            showLoading(adapter.itemCount == 0)
        }

        isLoading = true

        loadJob?.cancel()
        loadJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).getMeHistory(
                    page = currentPage,
                    perPage = perPage
                )

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    val items = if (reset) {
                        adapter.setItems(response.items)
                        response.items
                    } else {
                        val combined = adapter.getItems() + response.items
                        adapter.setItems(combined)
                        combined
                    }
                    updateCount(response.total)
                    hasMore = response.hasMore
                    showEmpty(items.isEmpty())
                } else {
                    hasMore = false
                    if (reset) showEmpty(true)
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null && reset) {
                    showEmpty(true)
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
                hasMore = false
            } finally {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    isLoading = false
                    showLoading(false)
                    binding.swipeRefresh.isRefreshing = false
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun removeFromHistory(video: ProfileVideo) {
        val previous = adapter.getItems()
        val newList = previous.filterNot { it.id == video.id }
        adapter.setItems(newList)
        updateCount(newList.size)
        if (newList.isEmpty()) showEmpty(true)

        removeJob?.cancel()
        removeJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).removeFromHistory(video.id)

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    updateCount(response.total)
                } else {
                    adapter.setItems(previous)
                    updateCount(previous.size)
                    if (previous.isNotEmpty()) showEmpty(false)
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Не удалось удалить", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    adapter.setItems(previous)
                    updateCount(previous.size)
                    if (previous.isNotEmpty()) showEmpty(false)
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun confirmClearHistory() {
        AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
            .setTitle("Очистить историю?")
            .setMessage("Вся история просмотров будет удалена. Это действие нельзя отменить.")
            .setPositiveButton("Очистить") { _, _ -> clearHistory() }
            .setNegativeButton("Отмена", null)
            .show()
    }

    private fun clearHistory() {
        clearJob?.cancel()
        clearJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).clearHistory()

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    adapter.setItems(emptyList())
                    updateCount(0)
                    showEmpty(true)
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "История очищена", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateCount(count: Int) {
        _binding?.historyCount?.text = if (count > 0) "$count видео" else ""
    }

    private fun showLoading(show: Boolean) {
        _binding?.historyLoading?.visibility = if (show) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
    }

    private fun showEmpty(show: Boolean) {
        _binding?.historyEmpty?.visibility = if (show) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        _binding?.swipeRefresh?.visibility = if (show) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
    }

    private fun openPlayer(pv: ProfileVideo) {
        val video = Video(
            id = pv.id,
            title = pv.title,
            description = "",
            thumbnail = pv.thumbnail,
            videoUrl = pv.videoUrl ?: "",
            audioUrl = pv.audioUrl,
            duration = pv.duration,
            views = pv.views,
            likes = 0,
            published = "",
            author = Author(id = pv.authorId, name = pv.authorName, avatar = ""),
            tags = null
        )
        (activity as? MainActivity)?.openPlayer(video)
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        removeJob?.cancel()
        clearJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/profile/HistoryCardAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ProfileVideo
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemHistoryCardBinding
import ru.asmr1.app.utils.formatDuration
import java.util.Locale

class HistoryCardAdapter(
    private var items: List<ProfileVideo> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (ProfileVideo) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<HistoryCardAdapter.HistoryViewHolder>() {

    fun setItems(newItems: List<ProfileVideo>) {
        items = newItems
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): HistoryViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemHistoryCardBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return HistoryViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: HistoryViewHolder, position: Int) {
        holder.bind(items[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class HistoryViewHolder(private val binding: ItemHistoryCardBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(video: ProfileVideo) {
            binding.histTitle.text = video.title
            binding.histMeta.text = formatViewsShort(video.views)

            if (video.thumbnail.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.histThumb.load(video.thumbnail) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                }
            }

            if (video.duration > 0) {
                binding.histDuration.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.histDuration.text = formatDuration(video.duration)
            } else {
                binding.histDuration.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(video) }
        }
    }

    private fun formatViewsShort(n: Int): String = when {
        n >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fM", n / 1_000_000.0)
        n >= 1_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1fK", n / 1_000.0)
        else -> "$n"
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/profile/EditPlaylistSheet.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.CountDownTimer
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialogFragment
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.UpdatePlaylistRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.SheetEditPlaylistBinding

class EditPlaylistSheet : BottomSheetDialogFragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_ID = "playlist_id"
        private const val ARG_TITLE = "playlist_title"
        private const val ARG_VISIBILITY = "playlist_visibility"

        fun newInstance(id: Int, title: String, visibility: String) = EditPlaylistSheet().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply {
                putInt(ARG_ID, id)
                putString(ARG_TITLE, title)
                putString(ARG_VISIBILITY, visibility)
            }
        }
    }

    /** Колбэк после успешного сохранения */
    var onSaved: ((Int, String, String) -> Unit)? = null

    /** Колбэк после удаления */
    var onDeleted: ((Int) -> Unit)? = null

    private var _binding: SheetEditPlaylistBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager
    private var playlistId = 0
    private var initialTitle = ""
    private var initialVisibility = "public"

    private var timer: CountDownTimer? = null
    private var isDeleting = false

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater, container: ViewGroup?, savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = SheetEditPlaylistBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        playlistId = requireArguments().getInt(ARG_ID, 0)
        initialTitle = requireArguments().getString(ARG_TITLE) ?: ""
        initialVisibility = requireArguments().getString(ARG_VISIBILITY) ?: "public"

        binding.etPlaylistTitle.setText(initialTitle)

        // Ручное переключение RadioButton (без RadioGroup)
        binding.rbPublic.isChecked = initialVisibility != "private"
        binding.rbPrivate.isChecked = initialVisibility == "private"

        binding.rbPublic.setOnClickListener {
            binding.rbPublic.isChecked = true
            binding.rbPrivate.isChecked = false
        }
        binding.rbPrivate.setOnClickListener {
            binding.rbPrivate.isChecked = true
            binding.rbPublic.isChecked = false
        }

        binding.btnSavePlaylist.setOnClickListener { save() }
        binding.btnDeletePlaylist.setOnClickListener { startDeleteCountdown() }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // СОХРАНИТЬ
    // ============================================================
    private fun save() {
        val title = binding.etPlaylistTitle.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (title.isEmpty()) {
            binding.etPlaylistTitle.error = "Введите название"
            return
        }
        val visibility = if (binding.rbPrivate.isChecked && !binding.rbPublic.isChecked) "private" else "public"

        val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
        imm?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(binding.etPlaylistTitle.windowToken, 0)

        binding.btnSavePlaylist.isEnabled = false
        binding.btnSavePlaylist.text = "Сохранение..."
        binding.editProgress.visibility = View.VISIBLE

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run { dismiss(); return@launch }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                    .updatePlaylist(playlistId, UpdatePlaylistRequest(title, visibility))

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success && response.playlist != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Сохранено", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    onSaved?.invoke(playlistId, response.playlist.title, response.playlist.visibility)
                    dismiss()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    resetSaveButton()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    resetSaveButton()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun resetSaveButton() {
        _binding?.btnSavePlaylist?.isEnabled = true
        _binding?.btnSavePlaylist?.text = "Сохранить"
        _binding?.editProgress?.visibility = View.GONE
    }

    // ============================================================
    // УДАЛЕНИЕ — С ТАЙМЕРОМ 5 СЕК
    // ============================================================
    private fun startDeleteCountdown() {
        if (isDeleting) return
        isDeleting = true
        binding.btnDeletePlaylist.isEnabled = false

        timer?.cancel()
        timer = object : CountDownTimer(5000L, 1000L) {
            override fun onTick(ms: Long) {
                val seconds = (ms / 1000) + 1
                _binding?.btnDeletePlaylist?.text = "Удалить через $seconds…"
            }

            override fun onFinish() {
                _binding?.btnDeletePlaylist?.text = "Удалить плейлист"
                _binding?.btnDeletePlaylist?.isEnabled = true
                isDeleting = false
                showDeleteConfirmDialog()
            }
        }.start()
    }

    private fun showDeleteConfirmDialog() {
        AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
            .setTitle("Удалить плейлист?")
            .setMessage("Все видео будут убраны из плейлиста. Это действие нельзя отменить.")
            .setPositiveButton("Да, удалить") { _, _ -> performDelete() }
            .setNegativeButton("Отмена", null)
            .show()
    }

    private fun performDelete() {
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run { dismiss(); return@launch }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).deletePlaylist(playlistId)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Плейлист удалён", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    onDeleted?.invoke(playlistId)
                    dismiss()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        timer?.cancel()
        timer = null
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/profile/PlaylistFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.os.Bundle
import android.util.Log
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Author
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ProfileVideo
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentPlaylistBinding

class PlaylistFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_PLAYLIST_ID = "playlist_id"
        private const val ARG_PLAYLIST_TITLE = "playlist_title"
        private const val ARG_PLAYLIST_TYPE = "playlist_type"

        fun newInstance(playlistId: Int, title: String, type: String) = PlaylistFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply {
                putInt(ARG_PLAYLIST_ID, playlistId)
                putString(ARG_PLAYLIST_TITLE, title)
                putString(ARG_PLAYLIST_TYPE, type)
            }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentPlaylistBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager
    private lateinit var adapter: PlaylistVideoAdapter

    private var playlistId: Int = 0
    private var playlistTitle: String = ""
    private var playlistType: String = "custom"
    private var playlistVisibility: String = "public"
    private var canEdit: Boolean = true

    private var loadJob: Job? = null
    private var deleteJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater, container: ViewGroup?, savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentPlaylistBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        playlistId = requireArguments().getInt(ARG_PLAYLIST_ID, 0)
        playlistTitle = requireArguments().getString(ARG_PLAYLIST_TITLE) ?: ""
        playlistType = requireArguments().getString(ARG_PLAYLIST_TYPE) ?: "custom"

        binding.playlistTitle.text = playlistTitle
        binding.playlistCount.text = ""

        binding.btnBack.setOnClickListener {
            parentFragmentManager.popBackStack()
        }

        // Иконку редактирования показываем только для custom (не для liked/watchlater)
        binding.btnEditPlaylist.visibility = View.GONE // скрыть до загрузки
        binding.btnEditPlaylist.setOnClickListener { openEditSheet() }

        setupRecycler()
        loadPlaylist()
    }

    private fun setupRecycler() {
        adapter = PlaylistVideoAdapter(
            items = mutableListOf(),
            canEdit = true,
            onClick = { pv -> openPlayerFromPlaylist(pv) },
            onDeleteSwipe = { pv, _ -> onSwipeDelete(pv) },
            onReorder = { list -> onSaveOrder(list) }
        )

        binding.playlistRecycler.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.playlistRecycler.adapter = adapter
        binding.playlistRecycler.setHasFixedSize(false)
        adapter.attachToRecyclerView(binding.playlistRecycler)
    }

    private fun loadPlaylist() {
        loadJob?.cancel()
        showLoading(true)

        loadJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                    showError("Нужна авторизация")
                    return@launch
                }

                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).getPlaylist(playlistId)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    binding.playlistTitle.text = response.playlist.title
                    playlistTitle = response.playlist.title
                    playlistType = response.playlist.type
                    canEdit = response.playlist.canEdit
                    playlistVisibility = response.playlist.visibility ?: "public"

                    updateCount(response.videos.size)

                    // Иконку редактирования показываем только для custom
                    binding.btnEditPlaylist.visibility =
                        if (playlistType == "custom") View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

                    adapter = PlaylistVideoAdapter(
                        items = response.videos.toMutableList(),
                        canEdit = response.playlist.canEdit,
                        onClick = { pv -> openPlayerFromPlaylist(pv) },
                        onDeleteSwipe = { pv, _ -> onSwipeDelete(pv) },
                        onReorder = { list -> onSaveOrder(list) }
                    )
                    binding.playlistRecycler.adapter = adapter
                    adapter.attachToRecyclerView(binding.playlistRecycler)

                    if (response.videos.isEmpty()) showEmpty(true) else showEmpty(false)
                } else {
                    showError("Не удалось загрузить плейлист")
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e("PlaylistFragment", "loadPlaylist error", e)
                if (_binding != null) showError("Ошибка: ${e.message}")
            } finally {
                if (_binding != null) showLoading(false)
            }
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // РЕДАКТИРОВАНИЕ
    // ============================================================
    private fun openEditSheet() {
        val sheet = EditPlaylistSheet.newInstance(playlistId, playlistTitle, playlistVisibility)

        sheet.onSaved = { _, newTitle, newVisibility ->
            playlistTitle = newTitle
            playlistVisibility = newVisibility
            _binding?.playlistTitle?.text = newTitle
        }

        sheet.onDeleted = { _ ->
            // Возвращаемся в профиль
            parentFragmentManager.popBackStack()
        }

        sheet.show(childFragmentManager, "edit_playlist")
    }

    // ============================================================
    // УДАЛЕНИЕ ВИДЕО
    // ============================================================
    private fun onSwipeDelete(video: ProfileVideo) {
        val current = adapter.getItems().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.id == video.id }
        adapter.setItems(current)
        updateCount(current.size)
        if (current.isEmpty()) showEmpty(true)

        deleteJob?.cancel()
        deleteJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                    .removeFromPlaylist(playlistId, video.id)

                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                if (!response.success) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Не удалось удалить", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun onSaveOrder(list: List<ProfileVideo>) {
        if (list.isEmpty()) return

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val orderIds = list.map { it.id }
                ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                    .reorderPlaylist(playlistId, ru.asmr1.app.data.ReorderRequest(order = orderIds))
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e("PlaylistFragment", "reorder error", e)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateCount(count: Int) {
        _binding?.playlistCount?.text = "$count видео"
    }

    private fun showLoading(show: Boolean) {
        _binding?.playlistLoading?.visibility = if (show) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
    }

    private fun showEmpty(show: Boolean) {
        _binding?.playlistEmpty?.visibility = if (show) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        _binding?.playlistRecycler?.visibility = if (show) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
    }

    private fun showError(msg: String) {
        if (_binding == null) return
        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), msg, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        showEmpty(true)
    }

    private fun openPlayerFromPlaylist(pv: ProfileVideo) {
        val video = Video(
            id = pv.id,
            title = pv.title,
            description = "",
            thumbnail = pv.thumbnail,
            videoUrl = pv.videoUrl ?: "",
            audioUrl = pv.audioUrl,
            duration = pv.duration,
            views = pv.views,
            likes = 0,
            published = "",
            author = Author(id = pv.authorId, name = pv.authorName, avatar = ""),
            tags = null
        )
        (activity as? MainActivity)?.openPlayer(video)
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        deleteJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/profile/ProfileFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.profile

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.EditText
import android.widget.RadioGroup
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialog
import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton
import coil.load
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.CreatePlaylistRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ProfileVideo
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentProfileBinding

class ProfileFragment : Fragment() {

    private var _binding: FragmentProfileBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager
    private lateinit var historyAdapter: HistoryCardAdapter
    private lateinit var playlistAdapter: PlaylistBigAdapter

    private var loadJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentProfileBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        setupRecyclers()
        setupMenu()
        loadProfile()
    }

    private fun setupRecyclers() {
        historyAdapter = HistoryCardAdapter { pv -> openPlayerFromProfile(pv) }
        binding.historyRecycler.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(
            requireContext(), LinearLayoutManager.HORIZONTAL, false
        )
        binding.historyRecycler.adapter = historyAdapter

        playlistAdapter = PlaylistBigAdapter { card -> openPlaylist(card) }
        binding.playlistsRecycler.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(
            requireContext(), LinearLayoutManager.HORIZONTAL, false
        )
        binding.playlistsRecycler.adapter = playlistAdapter
    }

    private fun setupMenu() {
        binding.historyAll.setOnClickListener {
            parentFragmentManager.beginTransaction()
                .replace(ru.asmr1.app.R.id.feed_container, HistoryFragment())
                .addToBackStack(null)
                .commit()
        }
        binding.rowSettings.setOnClickListener {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Настройки — скоро", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
        binding.rowFaq.setOnClickListener {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "FAQ — скоро", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
        binding.rowLogout.setOnClickListener {
            (activity as? MainActivity)?.logout()
        }

        binding.btnAddPlaylist.setOnClickListener {
            showCreatePlaylistDialog()
        }
    }

    private fun loadProfile() {
        loadJob?.cancel()
        loadJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: return@launch
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).getMeProfile()
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    binding.profileName.text = response.user.name
                    binding.profileEmail.text = response.user.email
                    if (response.user.avatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                        binding.profileAvatar.load(response.user.avatar) {
                            crossfade(true)
                            placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                            error(R.color.card_bg)
                        }
                    }

                    // ===== История =====
                    binding.historyWrap.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    if (response.history.isNotEmpty()) {
                        binding.historyRecycler.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                        binding.historyEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
                        historyAdapter.setItems(response.history)
                    } else {
                        binding.historyRecycler.visibility = View.GONE
                        binding.historyEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    }

                    // ===== Плейлисты: системные + кастомные =====
                    val playlists = mutableListOf<PlaylistCard>()

                    if (response.watchlater.count > 0) {
                        playlists.add(
                            PlaylistCard(
                                id = response.watchlater.playlistId,
                                title = "Смотреть позже",
                                count = response.watchlater.count,
                                type = "watchlater",
                                firstVideo = response.watchlater.firstVideo
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    if (response.liked.count > 0) {
                        playlists.add(
                            PlaylistCard(
                                id = response.liked.playlistId,
                                title = "Понравившиеся",
                                count = response.liked.count,
                                type = "liked",
                                firstVideo = response.liked.firstVideo
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    response.customPlaylists.forEach { custom ->
                        playlists.add(
                            PlaylistCard(
                                id = custom.id,
                                title = custom.title,
                                count = custom.count,
                                type = "custom",
                                firstVideo = custom.firstVideo,
                                visibility = custom.visibility
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    if (playlists.isNotEmpty()) {
                        binding.playlistsWrap.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                        playlistAdapter.setItems(playlists)
                    } else {
                        binding.playlistsWrap.visibility = View.GONE
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка загрузки: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ДИАЛОГ СОЗДАНИЯ ПЛЕЙЛИСТА
    // ============================================================
    private fun showCreatePlaylistDialog() {
        val dialog = BottomSheetDialog(requireContext())
        val sheet = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.sheet_create_playlist, null)
        dialog.setContentView(sheet)

        val etTitle = sheet.findViewById<EditText>(R.id.et_playlist_title)
        val rgVisibility = sheet.findViewById<RadioGroup>(R.id.rg_visibility)
        val btnCreate = sheet.findViewById<MaterialButton>(R.id.btn_create_playlist)
        val progress = sheet.findViewById<View>(R.id.create_progress)

        btnCreate.setOnClickListener {
            val title = etTitle.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
            if (title.isEmpty()) {
                etTitle.error = "Введите название"
                return@setOnClickListener
            }

            val visibility = if (rgVisibility.checkedRadioButtonId == R.id.rb_private) "private" else "public"

            val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
            imm?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(etTitle.windowToken, 0)

            btnCreate.isEnabled = false
            btnCreate.text = "Создание..."
            progress.visibility = View.VISIBLE

            viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
                try {
                    val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Нужна авторизация", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                        dialog.dismiss()
                        return@launch
                    }
                    val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                        .createPlaylist(CreatePlaylistRequest(title = title, visibility = visibility))

                    if (response.success && response.playlist != null) {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Плейлист создан", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                        dialog.dismiss()
                        loadProfile()
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Не удалось создать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                        btnCreate.isEnabled = true
                        btnCreate.text = "Создать"
                        progress.visibility = View.GONE
                    }
                } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                    throw e
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    btnCreate.isEnabled = true
                    btnCreate.text = "Создать"
                    progress.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            }
        }

        dialog.show()

        etTitle.requestFocus()
        etTitle.postDelayed({
            val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
            imm?.showSoftInput(etTitle, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
        }, 200)
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ОТКРЫТИЕ ПЛЕЙЛИСТА
    // ============================================================
    private fun openPlaylist(card: PlaylistCard) {
        if (card.id <= 0) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Плейлист недоступен", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }
        val fragment = PlaylistFragment.newInstance(card.id, card.title, card.type)
        parentFragmentManager.beginTransaction()
            .replace(R.id.feed_container, fragment)
            .addToBackStack(null)
            .commit()
    }

    private fun openPlayerFromProfile(pv: ProfileVideo) {
        val video = Video(
            id = pv.id,
            title = pv.title,
            description = "",
            thumbnail = pv.thumbnail,
            videoUrl = pv.videoUrl ?: "",
            audioUrl = pv.audioUrl,
            duration = pv.duration,
            views = pv.views,
            likes = 0,
            published = "",
            author = ru.asmr1.app.data.Author(id = pv.authorId, name = pv.authorName, avatar = ""),
            tags = null
        )
        (activity as? MainActivity)?.openPlayer(video)
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/download/Downloader.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.download

import android.content.Context
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * Движок загрузки. Качает во внутреннее хранилище приложения.
 */
class Downloader(private val context: Context) {

    private val client = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build()

    private val storage = DownloadStorage(context)

    /**
     * Скачать файл. При завершении сохраняет полные метаданные видео.
     */
    fun download(
        video: Video,
        type: DownloadedFile.Type,
        url: String,
        extension: String
    ): Flow<DownloadProgress> = flow {

        val existing = storage.getFile(video.id, type)
        if (existing != null) {
            emit(DownloadProgress(video.id, type, existing.size, existing.size, 100))
            return@flow
        }

        val dir = DownloadStorage.getDownloadsDir(context)
        val finalName = "${type.name.lowercase()}_${video.id}.${extension}"
        val tmpName = "${finalName}.part"
        val tmpFile = File(dir, tmpName)
        val finalFile = File(dir, finalName)

        if (tmpFile.exists()) tmpFile.delete()

        val request = Request.Builder()
            .url(url)
            .header("User-Agent", "ASMR1-Android/1.0")
            .build()

        client.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
            if (!response.isSuccessful) throw RuntimeException("HTTP ${response.code}")
            val body = response.body ?: throw RuntimeException("Empty body")

            val total = body.contentLength()
            var downloaded = 0L

            body.byteStream().use { input ->
                tmpFile.outputStream().use { output ->
                    val buffer = ByteArray(8 * 1024)
                    var lastEmit = 0L
                    while (true) {
                        val read = input.read(buffer)
                        if (read <= 0) break
                        output.write(buffer, 0, read)
                        downloaded += read

                        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                        if (now - lastEmit >= 200 || (total > 0 && downloaded >= total)) {
                            lastEmit = now
                            val percent = if (total > 0) {
                                ((downloaded.toDouble() / total) * 100).toInt().coerceIn(0, 100)
                            } else 0
                            emit(DownloadProgress(video.id, type, downloaded, if (total > 0) total else 0, percent))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (finalFile.exists()) finalFile.delete()
        val ok = tmpFile.renameTo(finalFile)
        if (!ok) {
            tmpFile.copyTo(finalFile, overwrite = true)
            tmpFile.delete()
        }

        storage.saveFile(video, type, finalFile)

        emit(DownloadProgress(video.id, type, finalFile.length(), finalFile.length(), 100))
    }.flowOn(Dispatchers.IO)

    fun delete(videoId: Int, type: DownloadedFile.Type): Boolean {
        return storage.deleteFile(videoId, type)
    }

    fun getRemoteFileSize(url: String): Long {
        return try {
            val request = Request.Builder()
                .url(url)
                .head()
                .header("User-Agent", "ASMR1-Android/1.0")
                .build()
            client.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
                if (!response.isSuccessful) return -1
                response.header("Content-Length")?.toLongOrNull() ?: -1
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            -1
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/download/DownloadStorage.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.download

import android.content.Context
import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import java.io.File

/**
 * Хранилище метаданных о скачанных файлах.
 * Файлы — context.filesDir/downloads/
 * Метаданные — SharedPreferences (JSON).
 *
 * Кэш индекса в памяти — чтобы не парсить JSON на каждый вызов hasVideo()/hasAudio().
 */
class DownloadStorage(context: Context) {

    private val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("asmr1_downloads", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private val gson = Gson()
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext

    companion object {
        private const val KEY_INDEX = "downloads_index"

        fun getDownloadsDir(context: Context): File {
            val dir = File(context.filesDir, "downloads")
            if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs()
            return dir
        }

        // ===== КЭШ ИНДЕКСА В ПАМЯТИ (ОБЩИЙ ДЛЯ ВСЕХ ЭКЗЕМПЛЯРОВ) =====
        @Volatile
        private var cachedIndex: MutableMap<String, DownloadedFile>? = null

        private fun invalidateCache() {
            cachedIndex = null
        }
    }

    private fun readIndex(): MutableMap<String, DownloadedFile> {
        // Если кэш есть — возвращаем копию (чтобы не мутировали снаружи)
        cachedIndex?.let { return HashMap(it) }

        val json = prefs.getString(KEY_INDEX, null) ?: run {
            val empty = mutableMapOf<String, DownloadedFile>()
            cachedIndex = empty
            return HashMap(empty)
        }

        val parsed = try {
            val type = object : TypeToken<MutableMap<String, DownloadedFile>>() {}.type
            gson.fromJson<MutableMap<String, DownloadedFile>>(json, type) ?: mutableMapOf()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            mutableMapOf()
        }

        cachedIndex = parsed
        return HashMap(parsed)
    }

    private fun writeIndex(index: Map<String, DownloadedFile>) {
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_INDEX, gson.toJson(index)).apply()
        // Обновляем кэш — теперь он актуален
        cachedIndex = HashMap(index)
    }

    private fun key(videoId: Int, type: DownloadedFile.Type): String =
        "${videoId}_${type.name.lowercase()}"

    // ===== Чтение =====

    fun getFile(videoId: Int, type: DownloadedFile.Type): DownloadedFile? {
        val index = readIndex()
        val entry = index[key(videoId, type)] ?: return null
        val f = File(getDownloadsDir(appContext), entry.fileName)
        return if (f.exists()) entry else {
            index.remove(key(videoId, type))
            writeIndex(index)
            null
        }
    }

    fun hasVideo(videoId: Int): Boolean = getFile(videoId, DownloadedFile.Type.VIDEO) != null
    fun hasAudio(videoId: Int): Boolean = getFile(videoId, DownloadedFile.Type.AUDIO) != null

    fun hasAnyDownloads(): Boolean = readIndex().isNotEmpty()

    fun getAllDownloads(): List<DownloadedFile> {
        val all = readIndex().values.filter { entry ->
            val f = File(getDownloadsDir(appContext), entry.fileName)
            f.exists()
        }
        return all.sortedByDescending { it.downloadedAt }
    }

    fun getDownloads(type: DownloadedFile.Type? = null): List<DownloadedFile> {
        val all = getAllDownloads()
        return if (type == null) all else all.filter { it.type == type }
    }

    // ===== Запись =====

    fun saveFile(video: Video, type: DownloadedFile.Type, file: File) {
        val index = readIndex()
        index[key(video.id, type)] = DownloadedFile(
            videoId = video.id,
            type = type,
            fileName = file.name,
            size = file.length(),
            downloadedAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
            title = video.title,
            description = video.description,
            thumbnail = video.thumbnail,
            duration = video.duration,
            views = video.views,
            likes = video.likes,
            published = video.published,
            videoUrl = video.videoUrl,
            audioUrl = video.audioUrl,
            authorId = video.author.id,
            authorName = video.author.name,
            authorAvatar = video.author.avatar
        )
        writeIndex(index)
    }

    fun deleteFile(videoId: Int, type: DownloadedFile.Type): Boolean {
        val entry = getFile(videoId, type) ?: return false
        val file = File(getDownloadsDir(appContext), entry.fileName)
        val deleted = if (file.exists()) file.delete() else true
        if (deleted) {
            val index = readIndex()
            index.remove(key(videoId, type))
            writeIndex(index)
        }
        return deleted
    }

    fun getFilePath(videoId: Int, type: DownloadedFile.Type): File? {
        val entry = getFile(videoId, type) ?: return null
        return File(getDownloadsDir(appContext), entry.fileName)
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/download/DownloadModels.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.download

import ru.asmr1.app.data.Author
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video

/**
 * Что скачано для конкретного видео.
 * Хранит все данные о видео, чтобы работал офлайн-просмотр.
 */
data class DownloadedFile(
    val videoId: Int,
    val type: Type,
    val fileName: String,
    val size: Long,
    val downloadedAt: Long,

    // ===== Полные данные видео для офлайн-режима =====
    val title: String = "",
    val description: String = "",
    val thumbnail: String = "",
    val duration: Int = 0,
    val views: Int = 0,
    val likes: Int = 0,
    val published: String = "",
    val videoUrl: String = "",
    val audioUrl: String? = null,
    val authorId: Int = 0,
    val authorName: String = "",
    val authorAvatar: String = ""
) {
    enum class Type { VIDEO, AUDIO }

    /**
     * Собрать объект Video для передачи в плеер.
     */
    fun toVideo(): Video {
        return Video(
            id = videoId,
            title = title,
            description = description,
            thumbnail = thumbnail,
            videoUrl = videoUrl,
            audioUrl = audioUrl,
            duration = duration,
            views = views,
            likes = likes,
            published = published,
            author = Author(
                id = authorId,
                name = authorName,
                avatar = authorAvatar
            ),
            tags = null
        )
    }
}

data class DownloadProgress(
    val videoId: Int,
    val type: DownloadedFile.Type,
    val bytesDownloaded: Long,
    val totalBytes: Long,
    val progressPercent: Int
) {
    val isDone: Boolean get() = progressPercent >= 100
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/download/PlaybackModeResolver.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.download

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import java.io.File

/**
 * Определяет, откуда играть видео:
 * - Скачано видео → локальный .mp4
 * - Скачано аудио → локальный .mp3 (в аудио-режиме)
 * - Ничего не скачано → онлайн URL
 */
object PlaybackModeResolver {

    sealed class Mode {
        /** Играем видео (локально или онлайн) */
        data class VideoMode(val uri: Uri) : Mode()

        /** Играем только аудио — вместо видео показываем превью */
        data class AudioMode(
            val uri: Uri,
            val previewUrl: String
        ) : Mode()
    }

    /**
     * @param video объект видео
     * @return Mode — как играть
     */
    fun resolve(context: Context, video: Video): Mode {
        android.util.Log.d("VideoTap", "t=${System.currentTimeMillis()} — resolver START")
        val storage = DownloadStorage(context)

        val hasVideo = storage.hasVideo(video.id)
        val hasAudio = storage.hasAudio(video.id)

        // Приоритет: локальное видео > локальное аудио > онлайн видео
        return when {
            hasVideo -> {
                val file = storage.getFilePath(video.id, DownloadedFile.Type.VIDEO)
                if (file != null && file.exists()) {
                    Mode.VideoMode(Uri.fromFile(file))
                } else {
                    // Файл пропал — играем онлайн
                    Mode.VideoMode(Uri.parse(video.videoUrl))
                }
            }
            hasAudio -> {
                val file = storage.getFilePath(video.id, DownloadedFile.Type.AUDIO)
                if (file != null && file.exists()) {
                    Mode.AudioMode(
                        uri = Uri.fromFile(file),
                        previewUrl = video.thumbnail
                    )
                } else {
                    Mode.VideoMode(Uri.parse(video.videoUrl))
                }
            }
            else -> {
                // Ничего не скачано — онлайн
                Mode.VideoMode(Uri.parse(video.videoUrl))
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Просто определить, играем ли мы локальный файл (полезно для UI).
     */
    fun isOffline(context: Context, videoId: Int): Boolean {
        val storage = DownloadStorage(context)
        return storage.hasVideo(videoId) || storage.hasAudio(videoId)
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/MainActivity.Mini.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.hypot

/**
 * Mini-плеер: жесты перетаскивания и pinch-to-zoom.
 *
 * Extension-функции для MainActivity.
 * Константы CARD_RATIO / CONTROLS_DP / CONTROLS_MARGIN_DP теперь top-level в MainActivity.kt.
 */

@SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
internal fun MainActivity.setupMiniPlayerGestures() {
    var startX = 0f
    var startY = 0f
    var startTouchX = 0f
    var startTouchY = 0f
    var isDragging = false
    var downTime = 0L

    var isPinching = false
    var pinchStartDist = 0f
    var pinchStartWidth = 0
    var pinchCenterX = 0f
    var pinchCenterY = 0f

    miniPlayerWrap.setOnTouchListener { v, event ->
        val parent = v.parent as? ViewGroup ?: return@setOnTouchListener false
        val parentW = parent.width.toFloat()
        val parentH = parent.height.toFloat()
        val marginF = marginPx.toFloat()
        val minWidthPx = (minWidthDp * density).toInt()
        val maxWidthPx = (maxWidthDp * density).toInt()

        when (event.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                startX = v.x
                startY = v.y
                startTouchX = event.rawX
                startTouchY = event.rawY
                isDragging = false
                downTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_DOWN -> {
                if (event.pointerCount >= 2) {
                    isPinching = true
                    isDragging = false
                    pinchStartDist = distanceBetween(event, 0, 1)
                    pinchStartWidth = v.width
                    pinchCenterX = v.x + v.width / 2f
                    pinchCenterY = v.y + v.height / 2f
                }
                true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                if (isPinching && event.pointerCount >= 2) {
                    val curDist = distanceBetween(event, 0, 1)
                    if (pinchStartDist > 0f && pinchStartWidth > 0) {
                        val factor = curDist / pinchStartDist
                        var newW = (pinchStartWidth * factor).toInt()
                        newW = newW.coerceIn(minWidthPx, maxWidthPx)
                        val newH = wrapHeightForWidth(newW)
                        val maxX = (parentW - newW - marginPx).coerceAtLeast(marginF)
                        val maxY = (parentH - newH - marginPx).coerceAtLeast(marginF)
                        val lp = v.layoutParams
                        lp.width = newW
                        lp.height = newH
                        v.layoutParams = lp
                        v.x = (pinchCenterX - newW / 2f).coerceIn(marginF, maxX)
                        v.y = (pinchCenterY - newH / 2f).coerceIn(marginF, maxY)
                    }
                    return@setOnTouchListener true
                }
                val maxX = (parentW - v.width - marginPx).coerceAtLeast(marginF)
                val maxY = (parentH - v.height - marginPx).coerceAtLeast(marginF)
                val dx = event.rawX - startTouchX
                val dy = event.rawY - startTouchY
                if (abs(dx) > 10 || abs(dy) > 10) isDragging = true
                v.x = (startX + dx).coerceIn(marginF, maxX)
                v.y = (startY + dy).coerceIn(marginF, maxY)
                true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_UP -> {
                if (isPinching) {
                    val idx = event.actionIndex
                    val remainIdx = if (idx == 0) 1 else 0
                    if (remainIdx < event.pointerCount) {
                        startTouchX = event.getRawX(remainIdx)
                        startTouchY = event.getRawY(remainIdx)
                        startX = v.x
                        startY = v.y
                    }
                    isPinching = false
                    isDragging = false
                }
                true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                if (!isDragging && System.currentTimeMillis() - downTime < 250) {
                    expandFromMini()
                } else if (isDragging) {
                    val maxX = (parentW - v.width - marginPx).coerceAtLeast(marginF)
                    val maxY = (parentH - v.height - marginPx).coerceAtLeast(marginF)
                    val cx = v.x + v.width / 2f
                    val cy = v.y + v.height / 2f
                    val targetX = if (cx < parentW / 2f) marginF else maxX
                    val targetY = if (cy < parentH / 2f) marginF else maxY
                    v.animate().x(targetX).y(targetY).setDuration(200)
                        .withEndAction { refreshPipSourceRect() }.start()
                }
                isDragging = false
                isPinching = false
                refreshPipSourceRect()
                true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                isDragging = false
                isPinching = false
                refreshPipSourceRect()
                true
            }
            else -> false
        }
    }

    val blockDrag = View.OnTouchListener { btn, ev ->
        when (ev.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> btn.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(true)
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> btn.parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(false)
        }
        false
    }
    miniBtnClose.setOnTouchListener(blockDrag)
    miniBtnPrev.setOnTouchListener(blockDrag)
    miniBtnPlayPause.setOnTouchListener(blockDrag)
    miniBtnNext.setOnTouchListener(blockDrag)
}

internal fun MainActivity.wrapHeightForWidth(width: Int): Int {
    val cardH = (width * CARD_RATIO).toInt()
    val controlsH = (CONTROLS_DP * density).toInt()
    val marginH = (CONTROLS_MARGIN_DP * density).toInt()
    return cardH + marginH + controlsH
}

internal fun MainActivity.distanceBetween(event: MotionEvent, i1: Int, i2: Int): Float {
    val dx = event.getX(i1) - event.getX(i2)
    val dy = event.getY(i1) - event.getY(i2)
    return hypot(dx, dy)
}
