package ru.asmr1.app.data

import android.os.Parcelable
import com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName
import kotlinx.parcelize.Parcelize

@Parcelize
data class AuthorPageResponse(
    @SerializedName("id")                val id: Int,
    @SerializedName("nickname")          val nickname: String,
    @SerializedName("avatar")            val avatar: String,
    @SerializedName("banner")            val banner: String?,
    @SerializedName("description")       val description: String?,
    @SerializedName("socials")           val socials: Socials,
    @SerializedName("subscribers_count") val subscribersCount: Int,
    @SerializedName("video_count")       val videoCount: Int,
    @SerializedName("total_views")       val totalViews: Int,
    @SerializedName("channel_joined")    val channelJoined: String?,
    @SerializedName("is_author")         val isAuthor: Boolean,
    @SerializedName("follow_status")     val followStatus: Boolean,
    @SerializedName("tabs")              val tabs: AuthorTabs?
) : Parcelable

@Parcelize
data class Socials(
    @SerializedName("youtube")         val youtube: String?,
    @SerializedName("telegram")        val telegram: String?,
    @SerializedName("vk")              val vk: String?,
    @SerializedName("rutube")          val rutube: String?,
    @SerializedName("dzen")            val dzen: String?,
    @SerializedName("boosty")          val boosty: String?,
    @SerializedName("patreon")         val patreon: String?,
    @SerializedName("donationalerts")  val donationalerts: String?,
    @SerializedName("website")         val website: String?
) : Parcelable

@Parcelize
data class AuthorTabs(
    @SerializedName("popular")           val popular: List<Video>,
    @SerializedName("videos")            val videos: List<Video>,
    @SerializedName("playlists")         val playlists: List<AuthorPlaylist>,
    @SerializedName("videos_pagination") val videosPagination: AuthorVideosPagination?
) : Parcelable

@Parcelize
data class AuthorVideosPagination(
    @SerializedName("page")     val page: Int,
    @SerializedName("per_page") val perPage: Int,
    @SerializedName("total")    val total: Int,
    @SerializedName("has_more") val hasMore: Boolean
) : Parcelable

@Parcelize
data class AuthorPlaylist(
    @SerializedName("id")         val id: Int,
    @SerializedName("title")      val title: String,
    @SerializedName("count")      val count: Int,
    @SerializedName("thumbnail")  val thumbnail: String?,
    @SerializedName("type")       val type: String,
    @SerializedName("visibility") val visibility: String?
) : Parcelable

// ============================================================
// Кастомный десериализатор для Socials: переваривает и {} и []
// ============================================================
object SocialsDeserializer : com.google.gson.JsonDeserializer<Socials> {
    override fun deserialize(
        json: com.google.gson.JsonElement?,
        typeOfT: java.lang.reflect.Type?,
        context: com.google.gson.JsonDeserializationContext?
    ): Socials {
        // Если сервер вернул пустой массив [] или null — отдаём пустой Socials
        if (json == null || json.isJsonNull || json.isJsonArray) {
            return Socials(null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null)
        }
        // Если объект — берём поля как обычно
        val obj = json.asJsonObject
        fun s(key: String) = obj.get(key)?.takeIf { !it.isJsonNull }?.asString
        return Socials(
            youtube        = s("youtube"),
            telegram       = s("telegram"),
            vk             = s("vk"),
            rutube         = s("rutube"),
            dzen           = s("dzen"),
            boosty         = s("boosty"),
            patreon        = s("patreon"),
            donationalerts = s("donationalerts"),
            website        = s("website")
        )
    }
}

import retrofit2.http.Body

interface AuthApiService {

    @POST("wp-json/jwt-auth/v1/token")
    suspend fun login(@Body body: LoginRequest): TokenResponse

    @POST("wp-json/jwt-auth/v1/token/validate")
    suspend fun validateToken(): ValidateResponse

    @GET("wp-json/wp/v2/users/me")
    suspend fun getCurrentUser(): User

    // ============================================================

    // ============================================================

    suspend fun getRegisterCaptcha(): CaptchaResponse

    @POST("wp-json/yt/v1/register")
    suspend fun register(@Body body: RegisterRequest): RegisterResponse
}

package ru.asmr1.app.data

import com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName

data class User(
    val id: Int,
    val name: String,
    val description: String?,
    val slug: String?,
    @SerializedName("avatar_urls") val avatarUrls: Map<String, String>?
) {
    val avatarUrl: String?
        get() = avatarUrls?.get("96") ?: avatarUrls?.get("48") ?: avatarUrls?.get("24")
}

    @SerializedName("code")    val code: String? = null,
    @SerializedName("message") val message: String? = null
)

import com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName

/**

    @SerializedName("captcha_id") val captchaId: String,
    @SerializedName("question") val question: String,
    @SerializedName("code") val code: String? = null,
    @SerializedName("message") val message: String? = null
)

 * Запрос POST /yt/v1/register

    @SerializedName("username") val username: String,
    @SerializedName("email") val email: String,
    @SerializedName("password") val password: String,
    @SerializedName("captcha_id") val captchaId: String,

    @SerializedName("message") val message: String?,
    @SerializedName("token") val token: String?,

    @SerializedName("user_nicename") val userNicename: String?,

package ru.asmr1.app.data

import retrofit2.http.Body
import retrofit2.http.GET
import retrofit2.http.POST
import retrofit2.http.Path
import retrofit2.http.Query

interface ApiService {

    @GET("feed")
    suspend fun getFeed(
        @Query("tab") tab: String = "recommended",
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20
    ): FeedResponse

    @GET("search")
    suspend fun search(
        @Query("q") query: String,
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20,
        @Query("orderby") orderBy: String = "weight",
        @Query("order") order: String = "DESC"
    ): SearchResponse

    @GET("subscriptions/authors")
    suspend fun getSubscribedAuthors(): SubscribedAuthorsResponse

    @GET("subscriptions/feed")
    suspend fun getSubscriptionsFeed(
        @Query("author_id") authorId: Int = 0,
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20
    ): SubscriptionsFeedResponse

    @POST("mark-viewed/{id}")
    suspend fun markAuthorViewed(@Path("id") authorId: Int): MarkViewedResponse

    @GET("like-status/{id}")
    suspend fun getLikeStatus(@Path("id") videoId: Int): LikeStatusResponse

    @POST("like/{id}")
    suspend fun toggleLike(@Path("id") videoId: Int): LikeResponse

    @GET("subscription/{id}")
    suspend fun getSubscriptionStatus(@Path("id") authorId: Int): SubscriptionStatusResponse

    @POST("subscribe/{id}")
    suspend fun subscribe(
        @Path("id") authorId: Int,
        @Body body: SubscribeRequest
    ): SubscribeResponse

    @GET("comments/{id}")
    suspend fun getComments(
        @Path("id") videoId: Int,
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20,
        @Query("order") order: String = "new"
    ): CommentsResponse

    @POST("comment/{id}")
    suspend fun postComment(
        @Path("id") videoId: Int,
        @Body body: CommentCreateRequest
    ): CommentCreateResponse

    @POST("comment-like/{id}")
    suspend fun toggleCommentLike(
        @Path("id") commentId: Int
    ): CommentLikeResponse

    @GET("comment/{id}/replies")
    suspend fun getCommentReplies(
        @Path("id") parentCommentId: Int,
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20
    ): CommentRepliesResponse

    @GET("notifications")
    suspend fun getNotifications(
        @Query("filter") filter: String = "all",
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20
    ): NotificationsResponse

    @POST("notifications/mark-read/{id}")
    suspend fun markNotificationRead(
        @Path("id") id: String
    ): NotificationReadResponse

    @POST("notifications/mark-all-read")
    suspend fun markAllNotificationsRead(): NotificationUnreadResponse

    @POST("notifications/delete/{id}")
    suspend fun deleteNotification(
        @Path("id") id: String
    ): NotificationReadResponse

    @GET("me/profile")
    suspend fun getMeProfile(): MeProfileResponse

    @GET("me/history")
    suspend fun getMeHistory(
        @Query("page") page: Int = 1,
        @Query("per_page") perPage: Int = 20
    ): MeHistoryResponse

    @POST("me/history/remove/{id}")
    suspend fun removeFromHistory(
        @Path("id") videoId: Int
    ): HistoryRemoveResponse

    @POST("me/history/clear")
    suspend fun clearHistory(): HistoryClearResponse

    @GET("playlist/{id}")
    suspend fun getPlaylist(
        @Path("id") playlistId: Int
    ): PlaylistFullResponse

    @POST("playlist/{id}/reorder")
    suspend fun reorderPlaylist(
        @Path("id") playlistId: Int,
        @Body body: ReorderRequest
    ): ReorderResponse

    @POST("playlist/{id}/remove/{video_id}")
    suspend fun removeFromPlaylist(
        @Path("id") playlistId: Int,
        @Path("video_id") videoId: Int
    ): RemoveFromPlaylistResponse

    @POST("playlist/create")
    suspend fun createPlaylist(
        @Body body: CreatePlaylistRequest
    ): CreatePlaylistResponse

    @POST("playlist/{id}/add/{video_id}")
    suspend fun addToPlaylist(
        @Path("id") playlistId: Int,
        @Path("video_id") videoId: Int
    ): AddToPlaylistResponse

    @GET("video/{id}/playlists")
    suspend fun getVideoPlaylists(
        @Path("id") videoId: Int
    ): VideoInPlaylistsResponse

    @POST("playlist/{id}/update")
    suspend fun updatePlaylist(
        @Path("id") playlistId: Int,
        @Body body: UpdatePlaylistRequest
    ): UpdatePlaylistResponse

    @POST("playlist/{id}/delete")
    suspend fun deletePlaylist(
        @Path("id") playlistId: Int
    ): DeletePlaylistResponse

    @GET("user/{id}")
    suspend fun getUserPage(
        @Path("id") userId: Int
    ): AuthorPageResponse

}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/data/ApiClient.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.data

import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.logging.HttpLoggingInterceptor
import retrofit2.Retrofit
import retrofit2.converter.gson.GsonConverterFactory
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthInterceptor
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

object ApiClient {

    private const val BASE_URL = "https://asmr1.ru/"

    private fun createCustomGson(): com.google.gson.Gson {
        return com.google.gson.GsonBuilder()
            .registerTypeAdapter(
                ru.asmr1.app.data.Socials::class.java,
                ru.asmr1.app.data.SocialsDeserializer
            )
            .create()
    }

    private val customGson by lazy { createCustomGson() }

    private val logging = HttpLoggingInterceptor().apply {
        level = HttpLoggingInterceptor.Level.BODY
    }

    private val baseClient: OkHttpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .addInterceptor(logging)
        .connectTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build()

    val service: ApiService by lazy {
        Retrofit.Builder()
            .baseUrl(BASE_URL + "wp-json/yt/v1/")
            .client(baseClient)
            .addConverterFactory(GsonConverterFactory.create(customGson))
            .build()
            .create(ApiService::class.java)
    }

    val authApi: AuthApiService by lazy {
        Retrofit.Builder()
            .baseUrl(BASE_URL)
            .client(baseClient)
            .addConverterFactory(GsonConverterFactory.create(customGson))
            .build()
            .create(AuthApiService::class.java)
    }

    fun createJwtService(token: String): ApiService {
        val jwtClient = baseClient.newBuilder()
            .addInterceptor(AuthInterceptor(token))
            .build()
        return Retrofit.Builder()
            .baseUrl(BASE_URL + "wp-json/yt/v1/")
            .client(jwtClient)
            .addConverterFactory(GsonConverterFactory.create(customGson))
            .build()
            .create(ApiService::class.java)
    }

    fun createJwtAuthService(token: String): AuthApiService {
        val jwtClient = baseClient.newBuilder()
            .addInterceptor(AuthInterceptor(token))
            .build()
        return Retrofit.Builder()
            .baseUrl(BASE_URL)
            .client(jwtClient)
            .addConverterFactory(GsonConverterFactory.create(customGson))
            .build()
            .create(AuthApiService::class.java)
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/CommentsFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.os.Bundle
import android.text.Editable
import android.text.TextWatcher
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.MainActivity
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Comment
import ru.asmr1.app.data.CommentCreateRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Video
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.FragmentCommentsBinding
import kotlin.math.abs

class CommentsFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        const val ARG_VIDEO = "video"
        private const val PER_PAGE = 20
        private const val DRAG_THRESHOLD = 150f

        fun newInstance(video: Video) = CommentsFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putParcelable(ARG_VIDEO, video) }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentCommentsBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: CommentsAdapter
    private lateinit var layoutManager: LinearLayoutManager
    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager

    private var video: Video? = null
    private var currentPage = 1
    private var isLoading = false
    private var hasMore = true
    private var order: String = "new"

    private var replyToComment: Comment? = null

    private var loadJob: Job? = null
    private var sendJob: Job? = null

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentCommentsBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        video = requireArguments().getParcelable(ARG_VIDEO) ?: return

        setupRecycler()
        setupSortButtons()
        setupInput()
        setupClose()
        setupDragHandle()

        loadComments(reset = true)
    }

    private fun applyPendingFocus() {
        val activity = activity as? MainActivity ?: return
        val focus = activity.pendingCommentFocus ?: return
        activity.pendingCommentFocus = null

        val comments = adapter.getComments()
        val idx = comments.indexOfFirst { it.id == focus.id }
        if (idx >= 0) {
            binding.commentsRecycler.postDelayed({
                binding.commentsRecycler.smoothScrollToPosition(idx)
            }, 150)
        }

        binding.commentInput.postDelayed({
            onReplyClick(focus)
        }, 250)
    }

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    private fun setupDragHandle() {
        val container = (requireActivity() as MainActivity).findViewById<View>(
            R.id.comments_overlay_container
        ) ?: return

        var startY = 0f
        var startTranslation = 0f
        var isDragging = false

        binding.handle.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            when (event.actionMasked) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    startY = event.rawY
                    startTranslation = container.translationY
                    isDragging = false
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - startY
                    if (!isDragging && abs(dy) > 10f) isDragging = true
                    if (isDragging) {
                        val newTranslation = (startTranslation + dy).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                        container.translationY = newTranslation
                    }
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP,
                MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - startY
                    if (isDragging && dy > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
                        (requireActivity() as MainActivity).closeComments()
                    } else {
                        container.animate()
                            .translationY(0f)
                            .setDuration(200)
                            .start()
                    }
                    isDragging = false
                    true
                }
                else -> false
            }
        }
    }

    private fun setupRecycler() {
        adapter = CommentsAdapter(
            onLikeClick = { comment -> onLikeClick(comment) },
            onReplyClick = { comment -> onReplyClick(comment) },
            onShowRepliesClick = { comment -> onShowRepliesClick(comment) }
        )
        layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.commentsRecycler.layoutManager = layoutManager
        binding.commentsRecycler.adapter = adapter
        binding.commentsRecycler.setHasFixedSize(false)
        binding.commentsRecycler.itemAnimator = null

        // Свайп вправо = Ответить
        binding.commentsRecycler.addOnItemTouchListener(
            SwipeReplyHelper(binding.commentsRecycler) { position ->
                val comment = adapter.getCommentAt(position)
                if (comment != null) onReplyClick(comment)
            }
        )

        binding.commentsRecycler.addOnScrollListener(object : RecyclerView.OnScrollListener() {
            override fun onScrolled(rv: RecyclerView, dx: Int, dy: Int) {
                super.onScrolled(rv, dx, dy)
                if (dy <= 0) return
                if (isLoading || !hasMore) return
                if (_binding == null) return

                val total = layoutManager.itemCount
                val lastVisible = layoutManager.findLastVisibleItemPosition()
                if (total > 0 && lastVisible >= total - 3) {
                    currentPage++
                    loadComments(reset = false)
                }
            }
        })
    }

    private fun setupSortButtons() {
        updateSortButtons()

        binding.sortNew.setOnClickListener {
            if (order == "new") return@setOnClickListener
            order = "new"
            updateSortButtons()
            loadComments(reset = true)
        }

        binding.sortTop.setOnClickListener {
            if (order == "top") return@setOnClickListener
            order = "top"
            updateSortButtons()
            loadComments(reset = true)
        }
    }

    private fun updateSortButtons() {
        val activeColor = ContextCompat.getColor(requireContext(), R.color.text_primary)
        val inactiveColor = ContextCompat.getColor(requireContext(), R.color.text_secondary)

        binding.sortNew.setTextColor(if (order == "new") activeColor else inactiveColor)
        binding.sortTop.setTextColor(if (order == "top") activeColor else inactiveColor)
    }

    private fun setupInput() {
        binding.commentSendBtn.setOnClickListener { sendComment() }

        binding.commentInput.addTextChangedListener(object : TextWatcher {
            override fun beforeTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, st: Int, c: Int, a: Int) {}
            override fun onTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, st: Int, b: Int, c: Int) {
                val has = !s.isNullOrBlank()
                binding.commentSendBtn.isEnabled = has
                binding.commentSendBtn.alpha = if (has) 1f else 0.5f
            }
            override fun afterTextChanged(s: Editable?) {}
        })
        binding.commentSendBtn.isEnabled = false
        binding.commentSendBtn.alpha = 0.5f
    }

    private fun sendComment() {
        val text = binding.commentInput.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (text.isEmpty()) return

        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы комментировать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }

        val v = video ?: return
        val parent = replyToComment
        val parentId = parent?.id ?: 0

        val draft = Comment(
            id = -System.currentTimeMillis().toInt(),
            content = text,
            authorId = authManager.getUserId(),
            authorName = authManager.getDisplayName() ?: "Вы",
            authorAvatar = "",
            date = "",
            dateGmt = "",
            likes = 0,
            liked = false,
            parentId = parentId,
            repliesCount = 0,
            replies = emptyList()
        )

        if (parent == null) {
            adapter.setComments(listOf(draft) + adapter.getComments())
        } else {
            val parentInList = adapter.getComments().firstOrNull { it.id == parent.id }
            if (parentInList != null) {
                val updatedParent = parentInList.copy(
                    replies = parentInList.replies + draft,
                    repliesCount = parentInList.repliesCount + 1
                )
                adapter.updateComment(updatedParent)
            }
        }

        binding.commentInput.text?.clear()
        replyToComment = null
        binding.commentInput.hint = "Написать комментарий..."
        binding.commentSendBtn.isEnabled = false
        binding.commentSendBtn.alpha = 0.5f

        val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(
            requireContext(),
            InputMethodManager::class.java
        )
        imm?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(binding.commentInput.windowToken, 0)

        sendJob?.cancel()
        sendJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                    removeDraft(draft)
                    return@launch
                }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).postComment(
                    v.id,
                    CommentCreateRequest(content = text, parentId = parentId)
                )

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success && response.comment != null) {
                    replaceDraft(draft, response.comment)
                } else {
                    removeDraft(draft)
                    Toast.makeText(
                        requireContext(),
                        response.message ?: "Не удалось отправить",
                        Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
                    ).show()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                removeDraft(draft)
                Toast.makeText(
                    requireContext(),
                    "Ошибка: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}",
                    Toast.LENGTH_LONG
                ).show()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun removeDraft(draft: Comment) {
        if (draft.parentId == 0) {
            val newList = adapter.getComments().filterNot { it.id == draft.id }
            adapter.setComments(newList)
        } else {
            val parentInList = adapter.getComments().firstOrNull { it.id == draft.parentId }
            if (parentInList != null) {
                val updatedParent = parentInList.copy(
                    replies = parentInList.replies.filterNot { it.id == draft.id },
                    repliesCount = (parentInList.repliesCount - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)
                )
                adapter.updateComment(updatedParent)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun replaceDraft(draft: Comment, real: Comment) {
        if (draft.parentId == 0) {
            val idx = adapter.getComments().indexOfFirst { it.id == draft.id }
            if (idx >= 0) {
                val newList = adapter.getComments().toMutableList()
                newList[idx] = real
                adapter.setComments(newList)
            }
        } else {
            val parentInList = adapter.getComments().firstOrNull { it.id == draft.parentId }
            if (parentInList != null) {
                val updatedReplies = parentInList.replies.map {
                    if (it.id == draft.id) real else it
                }
                val updatedParent = parentInList.copy(
                    replies = updatedReplies,
                    repliesCount = updatedReplies.size
                )
                adapter.updateComment(updatedParent)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun onReplyClick(comment: Comment) {
        replyToComment = comment
        binding.commentInput.hint = "Ответить ${comment.authorName}..."
        binding.commentInput.requestFocus()
        binding.commentInput.setSelection(binding.commentInput.text?.length ?: 0)

        val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(
            requireContext(),
            InputMethodManager::class.java
        )
        imm?.showSoftInput(binding.commentInput, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
    }

    private fun loadComments(reset: Boolean) {
        if (isLoading) return
        if (!hasMore && !reset) return

        val v = video ?: return
        if (reset) {
            currentPage = 1
            hasMore = true
            adapter.setComments(emptyList())
        } else {
            binding.commentsLoadingMore.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
        isLoading = true

        loadJob?.cancel()
        loadJob = viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken()
                val api = if (token != null) {
                    ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                } else {
                    ApiClient.service
                }
                val response = api.getComments(
                    videoId = v.id,
                    page = currentPage,
                    perPage = PER_PAGE,
                    order = order
                )

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    if (reset) {
                        adapter.setComments(response.comments)
                        binding.commentsEmpty.visibility =
                            if (response.comments.isEmpty()) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
                        applyPendingFocus()
                    } else {
                        adapter.appendComments(response.comments)
                    }
                    hasMore = response.hasMore
                } else {
                    if (reset) {
                        binding.commentsEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    }
                    hasMore = false
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                if (reset) {
                    binding.commentsEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                }
                hasMore = false
            } finally {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    isLoading = false
                    binding.commentsLoadingMore.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun onLikeClick(comment: Comment) {
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы лайкать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }

        val wasLiked = comment.liked
        val optimistic = comment.copy(
            liked = !wasLiked,
            likes = if (wasLiked) (comment.likes - 1).coerceAtLeast(0) else comment.likes + 1
        )
        adapter.updateComment(optimistic)

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                    adapter.updateComment(comment)
                    return@launch
                }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).toggleCommentLike(comment.id)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    val finalComment = comment.copy(
                        likes = response.likes,
                        liked = response.liked
                    )
                    adapter.updateComment(finalComment)
                } else {
                    adapter.updateComment(comment)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                adapter.updateComment(comment)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun onShowRepliesClick(comment: Comment) {
        val v = video ?: return
        val dialog = RepliesDialogFragment.newInstance(comment, v.id)
        dialog.show(childFragmentManager, "replies_dialog")
    }

    private fun setupClose() {
        binding.btnCommentsClose.setOnClickListener {
            (requireActivity() as MainActivity).closeComments()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        loadJob?.cancel()
        sendJob?.cancel()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/AddToPlaylistSheet.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialogFragment
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.CreatePlaylistRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.data.PlaylistCardItem
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.SheetAddToPlaylistBinding

class AddToPlaylistSheet : BottomSheetDialogFragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_VIDEO_ID = "video_id"
        const val RESULT_KEY = "playlist_added_result"

        fun newInstance(videoId: Int) = AddToPlaylistSheet().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putInt(ARG_VIDEO_ID, videoId) }
        }
    }

    /** Колбэк после успешного добавления (передаём videoId) */
    var onAdded: ((Int) -> Unit)? = null

    private var _binding: SheetAddToPlaylistBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager
    private lateinit var choiceAdapter: PlaylistChoiceAdapter

    private var videoId: Int = 0

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater, container: ViewGroup?, savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = SheetAddToPlaylistBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())
        videoId = requireArguments().getInt(ARG_VIDEO_ID, 0)

        choiceAdapter = PlaylistChoiceAdapter(
            items = emptyList(),
            containsIds = emptySet(),
            onClick = { item -> onPlaylistChosen(item) }
        )
        binding.rvPlaylists.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.rvPlaylists.adapter = choiceAdapter

        binding.rowCreateNew.setOnClickListener { showCreateForm() }
        binding.btnCreateAndAdd.setOnClickListener { createAndAdd() }

        loadPlaylists()
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ЗАГРУЗКА ПЛЕЙЛИСТОВ + ПРОВЕРКА ГДЕ ЛЕЖИТ ВИДЕО
    // ============================================================
    private fun loadPlaylists() {
        binding.listLoading.visibility = View.VISIBLE

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                    binding.listLoading.visibility = View.GONE
                    return@launch
                }
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)

                val profile = api.getMeProfile()
                val inWhich = try { api.getVideoPlaylists(videoId).playlistIds.toSet() } catch (_: Exception) { emptySet() }

                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                val items = mutableListOf<PlaylistCardItem>()

                if (profile.watchlater.count >= 0) {
                    items.add(
                        PlaylistCardItem(
                            id = profile.watchlater.playlistId,
                            title = "Смотреть позже",
                            count = profile.watchlater.count,
                            type = "watchlater",
                            visibility = "private"
                        )
                    )
                }
                if (profile.liked.count >= 0) {
                    items.add(
                        PlaylistCardItem(
                            id = profile.liked.playlistId,
                            title = "Понравившиеся",
                            count = profile.liked.count,
                            type = "liked",
                            visibility = "private"
                        )
                    )
                }
                profile.customPlaylists.forEach { c ->
                    items.add(
                        PlaylistCardItem(
                            id = c.id,
                            title = c.title,
                            count = c.count,
                            type = "custom",
                            visibility = c.visibility
                        )
                    )
                }

                if (items.isEmpty()) {
                    binding.tvEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                } else {
                    choiceAdapter.setContainsIds(inWhich)
                    choiceAdapter.setItems(items)
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } finally {
                if (_binding != null) binding.listLoading.visibility = View.GONE
            }
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ФОРМА СОЗДАНИЯ
    // ============================================================
    private fun showCreateForm() {
        binding.listContainer.visibility = View.GONE
        binding.createForm.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        binding.tvSheetTitle.text = "Новый плейлист"
        binding.etPlaylistTitle.requestFocus()
        binding.etPlaylistTitle.postDelayed({
            val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
            imm?.showSoftInput(binding.etPlaylistTitle, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
        }, 150)
    }

    // ============================================================
    // СОЗДАТЬ + ДОБАВИТЬ
    // ============================================================
    private fun createAndAdd() {
        val title = binding.etPlaylistTitle.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (title.isEmpty()) {
            binding.etPlaylistTitle.error = "Введите название"
            return
        }
        val visibility = if (binding.rgVisibility.checkedRadioButtonId == R.id.rb_private) "private" else "public"

        val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
        imm?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(binding.etPlaylistTitle.windowToken, 0)

        binding.btnCreateAndAdd.isEnabled = false
        binding.btnCreateAndAdd.text = "Создание..."
        binding.createProgress.visibility = View.VISIBLE

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run { dismiss(); return@launch }
                val api = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)

                val created = api.createPlaylist(CreatePlaylistRequest(title, visibility))
                if (!created.success || created.playlist == null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), created.message ?: "Не удалось создать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    resetButton()
                    return@launch
                }
                val added = api.addToPlaylist(created.playlist.id, videoId)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (added.success) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Добавлено в «$title»", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    onAdded?.invoke(videoId)
                    dismiss()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), added.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    resetButton()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    resetButton()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun resetButton() {
        _binding?.btnCreateAndAdd?.isEnabled = true
        _binding?.btnCreateAndAdd?.text = "Создать и добавить"
        _binding?.createProgress?.visibility = View.GONE
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ВЫБОР СУЩЕСТВУЮЩЕГО ПЛЕЙЛИСТА
    // ============================================================
    private fun onPlaylistChosen(item: PlaylistCardItem) {
        if (item.type == "liked") {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Понравившиеся формируются лайками", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run { dismiss(); return@launch }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                    .addToPlaylist(item.id, videoId)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    if (response.alreadyIn) {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Уже в «${item.title}»", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Добавлено в «${item.title}»", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                    onAdded?.invoke(videoId)
                    dismiss()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        _binding = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/CommentsAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.core.widget.ImageViewCompat
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DiffUtil
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import coil.request.CachePolicy
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Comment
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemCommentBinding
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

class CommentsAdapter(
    private var comments: List<Comment> = emptyList(),
    private val onLikeClick: (Comment) -> Unit = {},
    private val onReplyClick: (Comment) -> Unit = {},
    private val onShowRepliesClick: (Comment) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<CommentsAdapter.CommentViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).apply {
            timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        }
    }

    fun setComments(newComments: List<Comment>) {
        val old = comments
        val diff = DiffUtil.calculateDiff(object : DiffUtil.Callback() {
            override fun getOldListSize() = old.size
            override fun getNewListSize() = newComments.size
            override fun areItemsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o].id == newComments[n].id
            override fun areContentsTheSame(o: Int, n: Int) = old[o] == newComments[n]
        })
        comments = newComments
        diff.dispatchUpdatesTo(this)
    }

    fun appendComments(newComments: List<Comment>) {
        if (newComments.isEmpty()) return
        val startPos = comments.size
        comments = comments + newComments
        notifyItemRangeInserted(startPos, newComments.size)
    }

    fun updateComment(updated: Comment) {
        val idx = comments.indexOfFirst { it.id == updated.id }
        if (idx < 0) return
        val newList = comments.toMutableList()
        newList[idx] = updated
        comments = newList
        notifyItemChanged(idx)
    }

    fun getComments(): List<Comment> = comments

    fun getCommentAt(position: Int): Comment? = comments.getOrNull(position)

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): CommentViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemCommentBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return CommentViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: CommentViewHolder, position: Int) {
        holder.bind(comments[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = comments.size

    inner class CommentViewHolder(private val binding: ItemCommentBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(comment: Comment) {
            binding.commentAuthor.text = comment.authorName
            binding.commentDate.text = formatDate(comment.dateGmt)
            binding.commentText.text = comment.content

            if (comment.authorAvatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.commentAvatar.load(comment.authorAvatar) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                    memoryCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    diskCachePolicy(CachePolicy.ENABLED)
                    allowHardware(true)
                }
            }

            binding.commentLikeCount.text = if (comment.likes > 0) comment.likes.toString() else "0"
            binding.commentLikeIcon.setImageResource(
                if (comment.liked) R.drawable.ic_player_like_filled else R.drawable.ic_player_like
            )
            if (comment.liked) {
                ImageViewCompat.setImageTintList(
                    binding.commentLikeIcon,
                    ContextCompat.getColorStateList(binding.root.context, R.color.accent_red)
                )
                binding.commentLikeCount.setTextColor(
                    ContextCompat.getColor(binding.root.context, R.color.accent_red)
                )
            } else {
                ImageViewCompat.setImageTintList(
                    binding.commentLikeIcon,
                    ContextCompat.getColorStateList(binding.root.context, R.color.text_secondary)
                )
                binding.commentLikeCount.setTextColor(
                    ContextCompat.getColor(binding.root.context, R.color.text_secondary)
                )
            }

            binding.commentLikeBtn.setOnClickListener { onLikeClick(comment) }
            binding.commentReplyBtn.setOnClickListener { onReplyClick(comment) }

            if (comment.repliesCount > 0) {
                binding.commentShowRepliesRow.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.commentShowReplies.text = "Показать ответы (${comment.repliesCount})"
                binding.commentShowReplies.setOnClickListener { onShowRepliesClick(comment) }
            } else {
                binding.commentShowRepliesRow.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            binding.commentCard.translationX = 0f
        }
    }

    private fun formatDate(gmtDate: String): String {
        if (gmtDate.isEmpty()) return ""
        return try {
            val date = synchronized(dateFmt) { dateFmt.parse(gmtDate) } ?: return gmtDate
            val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
            when {
                diff < 60 -> "только что"
                diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60} мин назад"
                diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600} ч назад"
                diff < 2_592_000 -> "${diff / 86400} дн назад"
                diff < 31_536_000 -> "${diff / 2_592_000} мес назад"
                else -> "${diff / 31_536_000} г назад"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            gmtDate
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/PlayerActivity.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.app.PictureInPictureParams
import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.util.Rational
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.ImageButton
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import androidx.media3.common.MediaItem
import androidx.media3.common.Player
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.ExoPlayer
import androidx.media3.ui.PlayerView
import ru.asmr1.app.R

class PlayerActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    companion object {
        const val EXTRA_VIDEO_URL = "video_url"
        const val EXTRA_VIDEO_ID = "video_id"
        const val EXTRA_TITLE = "title"
        const val EXTRA_AUTHOR = "author"
        const val EXTRA_START_TIME_MS = "start_time_ms"
    }

    private lateinit var player: ExoPlayer
    private lateinit var playerView: PlayerView
    private var videoId: Int = 0
    private var startTimeMs: Long = 0L

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_player)

        window.setFlags(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_FULLSCREEN,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_FULLSCREEN
        )

        val videoUrl = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_VIDEO_URL) ?: ""
        videoId = intent.getIntExtra(EXTRA_VIDEO_ID, 0)
        startTimeMs = intent.getLongExtra(EXTRA_START_TIME_MS, 0L)

        findViewById<ImageButton>(R.id.btn_close).setOnClickListener {
            finish()
        }

        player = ExoPlayer.Builder(this).build()
        playerView = findViewById(R.id.player_view)
        playerView.player = player

        if (videoUrl.isNotEmpty()) {
            val mediaItem = MediaItem.fromUri(videoUrl)
            player.setMediaItem(mediaItem, startTimeMs)
            player.prepare()
            player.playWhenReady = true
        }

        setupAutoPip()

        player.addListener(object : Player.Listener {
            override fun onPlaybackStateChanged(state: Int) {
                if (state == Player.STATE_ENDED) {
                    finish()
                }
            }
        })
    }

    private fun setupAutoPip() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            try {
                val params = PictureInPictureParams.Builder()
                    .setAspectRatio(Rational(16, 9))
                    .setAutoEnterEnabled(true)
                    .setSeamlessResizeEnabled(true)
                    .build()
                setPictureInPictureParams(params)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onPictureInPictureModeChanged(
        isInPictureInPictureMode: Boolean,
        newConfig: Configuration
    ) {
        super.onPictureInPictureModeChanged(isInPictureInPictureMode, newConfig)
        findViewById<ImageButton>(R.id.btn_close).visibility =
            if (isInPictureInPictureMode) android.view.View.GONE else android.view.View.VISIBLE
    }

    override fun onStop() {
        super.onStop()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N && !isInPictureInPictureMode) {
            player.pause()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        player.release()
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/DownloadProgressView.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View

/**
 * Оверлей прогресса — заполняется снизу вверх полупрозрачным красным.
 */
class DownloadProgressView @JvmOverloads constructor(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null,
    defStyleAttr: Int = 0
) : View(context, attrs, defStyleAttr) {

    private var progress: Float = 0f
    private var visible: Boolean = false

    private val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.parseColor("#80CF001F")
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
    }

    private val rect = RectF()

    fun setProgress(p: Float) {
        progress = p.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        visible = progress > 0f && progress < 1f
        invalidate()
    }

    fun setProgressPercent(p: Int) {
        setProgress(p / 100f)
    }

    fun hide() {
        visible = false
        progress = 0f
        invalidate()
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        if (!visible) return

        val w = width.toFloat()
        val h = height.toFloat()
        val fillHeight = h * progress
        val top = h - fillHeight

        rect.set(0f, top, w, h)
        val radius = (w.coerceAtMost(h) * 0.2f)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(rect, radius, radius, paint)
    }
}

            val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
            when {
                diff < 60 -> "только что"
                diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60} мин назад"
                diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600} ч назад"
                diff < 2_592_000 -> "${diff / 86400} дн назад"
                diff < 31_536_000 -> "${diff / 2_592_000} мес назад"
                else -> "${diff / 31_536_000} г назад"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            gmtDate
        }
    }
}

package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.animation.ArgbEvaluator
import android.animation.ValueAnimator

import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo

import ru.asmr1.app.download.PlaybackModeResolver
