import kotlin.math.roundToInt

class PlayerFragment : Fragment() {

    companion object {
        const val ARG_VIDEO = "video"
        fun newInstance(video: Video) = PlayerFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply { putParcelable(ARG_VIDEO, video) }
        }
        private const val FS_SIDE_DP = 48f
        private const val FS_BOTTOM_DP = 45f
        private const val NORMAL_SIDE_DP = 12f
        private const val NORMAL_BOTTOM_TIME_DP = 12f
        private const val NORMAL_END_FS_DP = 8f

        private const val THUMB_SIZE_DP = 10f
        private const val PALETTE_BRIGHTNESS = 0.45f

        private const val RAPID_SEEK_WINDOW_MS = 1500L
    }

    private var _binding: FragmentPlayerBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private var controlsVisible = false
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    private var playerListener: Player.Listener? = null
    private var userSeeking = false

    private var isFullscreen = false
    private var gestureDetector: GestureDetector? = null
    private var isLongPressing = false

    private var isAudioMode = false

    private var accumulatedSeekMs = 0L
    private var lastSeekTapTime = 0L
    private val seekResetRunnable = Runnable {
        accumulatedSeekMs = 0L
    }

    private var rapidSeekActive = false
    private val rapidSeekResetRunnable = Runnable {
        rapidSeekActive = false
        accumulatedSeekMs = 0L
    }

    private val longPressRunnable = Runnable {
        isLongPressing = true
        MainActivity.globalPlayer?.setPlaybackSpeed(2.0f)
        showSpeedIndicator()
    }

    fun isFullscreenMode(): Boolean = isFullscreen

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = FragmentPlayerBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    private fun isPointInsidePlayerView(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        val view = binding.playerView
        val loc = IntArray(2)
        view.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
        val x = e.rawX
        val y = e.rawY
        return x >= loc[0] && x <= loc[0] + view.width &&
                y >= loc[1] && y <= loc[1] + view.height
    }

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        val screenWidth = resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels
        val targetHeight = (screenWidth * 9f / 16f).toInt()

        binding.playerFrame.layoutParams = binding.playerFrame.layoutParams.apply {
            height = targetHeight
        }
        binding.playerView.layoutParams = binding.playerView.layoutParams.apply {
            height = targetHeight
        }
        binding.audioPreview.layoutParams = binding.audioPreview.layoutParams.apply {
            height = targetHeight
        }

        binding.playerView.player = MainActivity.globalPlayer

        // ===== ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ РЕЖИМА ВОСПРОИЗВЕДЕНИЯ =====
        val video = requireArguments().getParcelable<Video>(ARG_VIDEO)
        if (video != null) {
            val mode = PlaybackModeResolver.resolve(requireContext(), video)
            when (mode) {
                is PlaybackModeResolver.Mode.AudioMode -> {
                    isAudioMode = true
                    binding.playerView.visibility = View.GONE
                    binding.audioPreview.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    binding.audioDim.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    binding.audioModeIcon.visibility = View.VISIBLE

                    // Загружаем превью
                    if (mode.previewUrl.isNotEmpty()) {
                        binding.audioPreview.load(mode.previewUrl) {
                            crossfade(true)
                            placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                            error(R.color.card_bg)
                        }
                    }
                }
                is PlaybackModeResolver.Mode.VideoMode -> {
                    isAudioMode = false
                    binding.playerView.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    binding.audioPreview.visibility = View.GONE
                    binding.audioDim.visibility = View.GONE
                    binding.audioModeIcon.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            }
        }

        val activity = requireActivity() as MainActivity

        binding.btnClose.setOnClickListener {
            if (isFullscreen) {
                exitFullscreen()
                binding.playerView.postDelayed({
                    activity.closePlayer()
                }, 250)
            } else {
                activity.closePlayer()
            }
        }

        binding.btnMinimize.setOnClickListener {
            if (isFullscreen) {
                exitFullscreen()
                binding.playerView.postDelayed({
                    activity.collapseToMini()
                }, 250)
            } else {
                activity.collapseToMini()
            }
        }

        binding.playerRoot.isClickable = true

        setupGestures()
        setupProgressBar()
        setupPlayerControls()
        setupTapAndSwipe()

        binding.controlsLayer.alpha = 0f
        binding.controlsLayer.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
        controlsVisible = false

        applyFullscreenProgressMargins(false)
        applyFullscreenControlsMargins(false)
        binding.progressBar.setCornersEnabled(false)

        binding.progressBar.setExpanded(false)
        binding.thumbView.visibility = View.GONE

        val thumbSizePx = (THUMB_SIZE_DP * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
        val halfThumb = thumbSizePx / 2f

        binding.progressBar.onThumbPosition = { cx, cy ->
            val pbLeft = binding.progressBar.left.toFloat()
            val pbTop = binding.progressBar.top.toFloat()
            binding.thumbView.x = pbLeft + cx - halfThumb
            binding.thumbView.y = pbTop + cy - halfThumb
        }

        binding.progressBar.post {
            binding.progressBar.setProgress(0f)
        }

        loadPaletteFromThumbnail()
    }

    private fun loadPaletteFromThumbnail() {
        val video = requireArguments().getParcelable<Video>(ARG_VIDEO) ?: return
        val url = video.thumbnail
        if (url.isEmpty()) return

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            val bitmap: Bitmap? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                try {
                    val request = ImageRequest.Builder(requireContext())
                        .data(url)
                        .size(80, 80)
                        .allowHardware(false)
                        .build()
                    val result = requireContext().imageLoader.execute(request)
                    (result.drawable as? BitmapDrawable)?.bitmap
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    null
                }
            }
            if (bitmap == null) return@launch

            val palette = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
                Palette.from(bitmap).generate()
            }

            val vibrant = palette.getVibrantColor(
                palette.getDominantColor(Color.BLACK)
            )
            val muted = palette.getMutedColor(
                palette.getDarkVibrantColor(Color.BLACK)
            )

            val darkVibrant = blendWithBlack(vibrant, PALETTE_BRIGHTNESS)
            val darkMuted = blendWithBlack(muted, PALETTE_BRIGHTNESS * 0.6f)

            val gradient = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable(
                android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable.Orientation.TOP_BOTTOM,
                intArrayOf(darkVibrant, darkMuted)
            )

            val current = (binding.playerFrame.background as? android.graphics.drawable.ColorDrawable)?.color
                ?: Color.BLACK
            val anim = ValueAnimator.ofObject(ArgbEvaluator(), current, darkVibrant).apply {
                duration = 400L
                addUpdateListener { a ->
                    val c = a.animatedValue as Int
                    binding.playerFrame.setBackgroundColor(c)
                }
                addListener(object : android.animation.AnimatorListenerAdapter() {
                    override fun onAnimationEnd(animation: android.animation.Animator) {
                        binding.playerFrame.background = gradient
                    }
                })
            }
            anim.start()
        }
    }

    private fun blendWithBlack(color: Int, factor: Float): Int {
        val r = (Color.red(color) * factor).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)
        val g = (Color.green(color) * factor).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)
        val b = (Color.blue(color) * factor).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)
        return Color.rgb(r, g, b)
    }

    fun hideControlsForStart() {
        handler.removeCallbacks(hideRunnable)
        binding.controlsLayer.animate().cancel()
        binding.controlsLayer.alpha = 0f
        binding.controlsLayer.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
        controlsVisible = false
        binding.progressBar.setExpanded(false)
        binding.thumbView.visibility = View.GONE
    }

    private fun showControls() {
        if (controlsVisible) return
        controlsVisible = true

        handler.removeCallbacks(hideRunnable)
        binding.controlsLayer.animate().cancel()

        binding.controlsLayer.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        binding.controlsLayer.animate()
            .alpha(1f)
            .setDuration(200)
            .start()

        if (isFullscreen) {
            binding.progressBar.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
        binding.progressBar.setExpanded(true)
        binding.thumbView.visibility = View.VISIBLE

        scheduleHide()
    }

    private fun hideControls() {
        if (!controlsVisible) return
        if (userSeeking) return
        controlsVisible = false

        handler.removeCallbacks(hideRunnable)
        binding.controlsLayer.animate().cancel()

        binding.controlsLayer.animate()
            .alpha(0f)
            .setDuration(200)
            .withEndAction {
                if (!controlsVisible) {
                    binding.controlsLayer.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
                }
            }
            .start()

        binding.progressBar.setExpanded(false)
        binding.thumbView.visibility = View.GONE

        if (isFullscreen) {
            binding.progressBar.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
        }
    }

    private fun toggleControls() {
        if (controlsVisible) hideControls() else showControls()
    }

    private val hideRunnable = Runnable { hideControls() }

    private fun scheduleHide() {
        handler.removeCallbacks(hideRunnable)
        handler.postDelayed(hideRunnable, 3500)
    }

    private fun setupGestures() {
        gestureDetector = GestureDetector(requireContext(), object : GestureDetector.SimpleOnGestureListener() {

            override fun onDown(e: MotionEvent): Boolean = true

            override fun onSingleTapConfirmed(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (!isPointInsidePlayerView(e)) return true

                if (rapidSeekActive) {
                    val player = MainActivity.globalPlayer ?: return true
                    val w = binding.playerView.width
                    val half = w / 2
                    val isLeft = e.x < half
                    val deltaMs = if (isLeft) -10_000L else 10_000L

                    val newPos = if (isLeft) {
                        (player.currentPosition - 10_000).coerceAtLeast(0)
                    } else {
                        (player.currentPosition + 10_000).coerceAtMost(player.duration)
                    }
                    player.seekTo(newPos)

                    showSeekEffect(e.x, isLeft, deltaMs)

                    handler.removeCallbacks(rapidSeekResetRunnable)
                    handler.postDelayed(rapidSeekResetRunnable, RAPID_SEEK_WINDOW_MS)

                    scheduleHide()
                    return true
                }

                toggleControls()
                return true
            }

            override fun onDoubleTap(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (!isPointInsidePlayerView(e)) return false
                val player = MainActivity.globalPlayer ?: return false
                val w = binding.playerView.width
                val half = w / 2
                val isLeft = e.x < half
                val deltaMs = if (isLeft) -10_000L else 10_000L

                val newPos = if (isLeft) {
                    (player.currentPosition - 10_000).coerceAtLeast(0)
                } else {
                    (player.currentPosition + 10_000).coerceAtMost(player.duration)
                }
                player.seekTo(newPos)

                showSeekEffect(e.x, isLeft, deltaMs)

                rapidSeekActive = true
                handler.removeCallbacks(rapidSeekResetRunnable)
                handler.postDelayed(rapidSeekResetRunnable, RAPID_SEEK_WINDOW_MS)

                scheduleHide()
                return true
            }
        })
    }

    private fun showSeekEffect(touchX: Float, isLeft: Boolean, deltaMs: Long) {
        binding.rippleView.start(touchX, binding.rippleView.height / 2f)

        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (now - lastSeekTapTime > 1000L) {
            accumulatedSeekMs = 0L
        }
        lastSeekTapTime = now
        accumulatedSeekMs += deltaMs

        handler.removeCallbacks(seekResetRunnable)

        val totalSec = (accumulatedSeekMs / 1000.0).roundToInt()
        val prefix = if (totalSec >= 0) "+" else "−"
        val absSec = Math.abs(totalSec)

        binding.seekIndicatorText.text = prefix + absSec + " сек"

        handler.postDelayed(seekResetRunnable, 1000)

        binding.seekIndicator.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        binding.seekIndicator.alpha = 1f
        binding.seekIndicator.animate().cancel()
        binding.seekIndicator.animate()
            .alpha(0f)
            .setStartDelay(700)
            .setDuration(300)
            .withEndAction { binding.seekIndicator.visibility = View.GONE }
            .start()
    }

    private fun showSpeedIndicator() {
        binding.seekIndicatorText.text = "x2"

        binding.seekIndicator.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        binding.seekIndicator.alpha = 1f
        binding.seekIndicator.animate().cancel()

        binding.rippleView.start(
            binding.rippleView.width / 2f,
            binding.rippleView.height / 2f
        )
    }

    private fun startLongPress() {
        handler.postDelayed(longPressRunnable, 500L)
    }

    private fun stopLongPress() {
        handler.removeCallbacks(longPressRunnable)
        if (isLongPressing) {
            isLongPressing = false
            MainActivity.globalPlayer?.setPlaybackSpeed(1.0f)
            binding.seekIndicator.animate()
                .alpha(0f)
                .setDuration(200)
                .withEndAction { binding.seekIndicator.visibility = View.GONE }
                .start()
        }
    }

    private fun setupProgressBar() {
        binding.progressBar.onSeekStart = {
            userSeeking = true
            handler.removeCallbacks(hideRunnable)
            binding.progressBar.setExpanded(true)
            binding.thumbView.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
        binding.progressBar.onSeek = { p ->
            val player = MainActivity.globalPlayer
            if (player != null) {
                val duration = player.duration
                if (duration > 0) {
                    val newPos = (duration * p).toLong()
                    player.seekTo(newPos)
                    binding.currentTime.text = formatTime(newPos)
                }
            }
        }
        binding.progressBar.onSeekEnd = {
            userSeeking = false
            if (controlsVisible) {
                scheduleHide()
            } else {
                binding.progressBar.setExpanded(false)
                binding.thumbView.visibility = View.GONE
            }
        }
    }

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    private fun setupTapAndSwipe() {
        var touchStartY = 0f
        var touchStartX = 0f
        var movedEnoughForSwipe = false
        var movedUpForFullscreen = false
        var movedBackSwipe = false
        var downInsidePlayer = false

        val backSwipeThresholdPx = (80 * resources.displayMetrics.density)

        binding.playerRoot.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            gestureDetector?.onTouchEvent(event)

            when (event.action) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    touchStartY = event.rawY
                    touchStartX = event.rawX
                    movedEnoughForSwipe = false
                    movedUpForFullscreen = false
                    movedBackSwipe = false
                    downInsidePlayer = isPointInsidePlayerView(event)

                    if (downInsidePlayer) startLongPress()

                    false
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - touchStartY
                    val dx = event.rawX - touchStartX

                    if (abs(dy) > 20 || abs(dx) > 20) {
                        stopLongPress()
                    }

                    if (isFullscreen && dx > backSwipeThresholdPx && dx > abs(dy) * 1.5f) {
                        movedBackSwipe = true
                        return@setOnTouchListener true
                    }

                    if (dy < -50 && abs(dy) > abs(dx) * 1.5f && !isFullscreen) {
                        movedUpForFullscreen = true
                    }

                    if (dy > 30 && dy > abs(dx) * 1.5f) {
                        movedEnoughForSwipe = true
                        (requireActivity() as MainActivity).onPlayerSwipeDown(dy)
                    }

                    movedEnoughForSwipe || movedUpForFullscreen || movedBackSwipe
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                    stopLongPress()

                    if (movedBackSwipe) {
                        handleBack()
                        true
                    } else if (movedUpForFullscreen && !isFullscreen) {
                        enterFullscreen()
                        true
                    } else if (movedEnoughForSwipe) {
                        val deltaY = event.rawY - touchStartY
                        if (deltaY > 150) {
                            val activity = requireActivity() as MainActivity
                            if (isFullscreen) {
                                exitFullscreen()
                                binding.playerView.postDelayed({
                                    activity.collapseToMini()
                                }, 250)
                            } else {
                                activity.collapseToMini()
                            }
                        } else {
                            (requireActivity() as MainActivity).onPlayerSwipeDown(0f)
                        }
                        true
                    } else {
                        false
                    }
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    stopLongPress()
                    false
                }
                else -> false
            }
        }
    }

    fun handleBack(): Boolean {
        if (isFullscreen) {
            exitFullscreen()
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun applyFullscreenProgressMargins(fullscreen: Boolean) {
        val lp = binding.progressBar.layoutParams as? ViewGroup.MarginLayoutParams ?: return
        val side = if (fullscreen) (FS_SIDE_DP * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt() else 0
        val bottom = if (fullscreen) (FS_BOTTOM_DP * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt() else 0
        lp.marginStart = side
        lp.marginEnd = side
        lp.bottomMargin = bottom
        binding.progressBar.layoutParams = lp
        binding.progressBar.requestLayout()
        binding.progressBar.setCornersEnabled(fullscreen)
    }

    private fun applyFullscreenControlsMargins(fullscreen: Boolean) {
        val dm = resources.displayMetrics.density

        val lpTime = binding.timeRow.layoutParams as? ViewGroup.MarginLayoutParams
        if (lpTime != null) {
            lpTime.marginStart = if (fullscreen) (FS_SIDE_DP * dm).toInt()
                              else (NORMAL_SIDE_DP * dm).toInt()
            lpTime.bottomMargin = (NORMAL_BOTTOM_TIME_DP * dm).toInt()
            binding.timeRow.layoutParams = lpTime
            binding.timeRow.requestLayout()
        }

        val lpFs = binding.btnFullscreen.layoutParams as? ViewGroup.MarginLayoutParams
        if (lpFs != null) {
            lpFs.marginEnd = if (fullscreen) (FS_SIDE_DP * dm).toInt()
                             else (NORMAL_END_FS_DP * dm).toInt()
            lpFs.bottomMargin = (NORMAL_BOTTOM_TIME_DP * dm).toInt()
            binding.btnFullscreen.layoutParams = lpFs
            binding.btnFullscreen.requestLayout()
        }
    }

    private fun enterFullscreen() {
        isFullscreen = true
        binding.btnFullscreen.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_player_exit_fullscreen)
        requireActivity().requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_LANDSCAPE

        val activity = requireActivity()
        val decor = activity.window.decorView
        decor.systemUiVisibility = (
            View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_FULLSCREEN
            or View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_HIDE_NAVIGATION
            or View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_IMMERSIVE_STICKY
        )

        binding.playerFrame.layoutParams = binding.playerFrame.layoutParams.apply {
            height = ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
        }
        binding.playerView.layoutParams = binding.playerView.layoutParams.apply {
            height = ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
        }
        binding.audioPreview.layoutParams = binding.audioPreview.layoutParams.apply {
            height = ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
        }
        binding.playerFrame.requestLayout()
        binding.playerView.requestLayout()
        binding.audioPreview.requestLayout()

        applyFullscreenProgressMargins(true)
        applyFullscreenControlsMargins(true)

        binding.progressBar.setExpanded(false)
        binding.progressBar.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
        binding.thumbView.visibility = View.GONE

        (requireActivity() as MainActivity).hideContentForFullscreen(true)
        showControls()
    }

    private fun exitFullscreen() {
        isFullscreen = false
        binding.btnFullscreen.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_player_fullscreen)
        requireActivity().requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT

        val activity = requireActivity()
        val decor = activity.window.decorView
        decor.systemUiVisibility = View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_VISIBLE

        applyFullscreenProgressMargins(false)
        applyFullscreenControlsMargins(false)

        binding.progressBar.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        binding.progressBar.setExpanded(false)
        binding.thumbView.visibility = View.GONE

        binding.playerFrame.postDelayed({
            val screenWidth = resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels
            val targetHeight = (screenWidth * 9f / 16f).toInt()
            binding.playerFrame.layoutParams = binding.playerFrame.layoutParams.apply {
                height = targetHeight
            }
            binding.playerView.layoutParams = binding.playerView.layoutParams.apply {
                height = targetHeight
            }
            binding.audioPreview.layoutParams = binding.audioPreview.layoutParams.apply {
                height = targetHeight
            }
            binding.playerFrame.requestLayout()
            binding.playerView.requestLayout()
            binding.audioPreview.requestLayout()
        }, 150)

        (requireActivity() as MainActivity).hideContentForFullscreen(false)
        showControls()
    }

    private fun setupPlayerControls() {
        val player = MainActivity.globalPlayer ?: return

        binding.btnPlayPause.setOnClickListener {
            if (player.isPlaying) player.pause() else player.play()
            scheduleHide()
        }

        binding.btnPrev.setOnClickListener { scheduleHide() }
        binding.btnNext.setOnClickListener { scheduleHide() }
        binding.btnFullscreen.setOnClickListener {
            if (isFullscreen) exitFullscreen() else enterFullscreen()
            scheduleHide()
        }

        playerListener = object : Player.Listener {
            override fun onIsPlayingChanged(isPlaying: Boolean) {
                if (_binding == null) return
                binding.btnPlayPause.setImageResource(
                    if (isPlaying) R.drawable.ic_player_pause else R.drawable.ic_player_play
                )
            }
        }
        player.addListener(playerListener!!)

        val runnable = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                if (_binding != null) {
                    updateTimes()
                    handler.postDelayed(this, 500)
                }
            }
        }
        handler.post(runnable)

        binding.btnPlayPause.setImageResource(
            if (player.isPlaying) R.drawable.ic_player_pause else R.drawable.ic_player_play
        )
        updateTimes()
    }

    private fun updateTimes() {
        if (_binding == null) return
        if (userSeeking) return
        val player = MainActivity.globalPlayer ?: return

        val duration = player.duration.coerceAtLeast(0L)
        val position = player.currentPosition.coerceAtLeast(0L)

        binding.currentTime.text = formatTime(position)
        binding.totalTime.text = formatTime(duration)

        if (duration > 0) {
            binding.progressBar.setProgress(position.toFloat() / duration.toFloat())
            binding.progressBar.setBuffered(player.bufferedPosition.toFloat() / duration.toFloat())
        } else {
            binding.progressBar.setProgress(0f)
            binding.progressBar.setBuffered(0f)
        }
    }

    private fun formatTime(ms: Long): String {
        if (ms <= 0) return "00:00"
        val totalSec = ms / 1000
        val h = totalSec / 3600
        val m = (totalSec % 3600) / 60
        val s = totalSec % 60
        return if (h > 0) {
            String.format("%d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s)
        } else {
            String.format("%02d:%02d", m, s)
        }
    }

    fun expandForPip() { /* no-op */ }
    fun restoreAfterPip() { /* no-op */ }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        handler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)
        playerListener?.let { MainActivity.globalPlayer?.removeListener(it) }
        playerListener = null
        binding.playerView.player = null
        _binding = null
        super.onDestroyView()
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/VideoProgressBar.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View

/**
 * Только полоса прогресса. Thumb рисуется отдельным View поверх (thumb_view).
 * Сообщает координаты thumb через [onThumbPosition].
 */
class VideoProgressBar @JvmOverloads constructor(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null,
    defStyleAttr: Int = 0
) : View(context, attrs, defStyleAttr) {

    private var progress = 0f
    private var buffered = 0f
    private var expanded = false
    private var cornersEnabled = false

    private val thinHeightPx = dpToPx(2f)
    private val thickHeightPx = dpToPx(3.5f)
    private val cornerPx = dpToPx(2f)

    // Высота зоны кликов
    private val touchAreaPx = dpToPx(24f)

    private val bgColor = Color.parseColor("#66000000")
    private val progressColor = Color.WHITE

    private val bgPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = bgColor
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
    }
    private val progressPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = progressColor
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
    }

    private val tmpRect = RectF()

    var onSeek: ((Float) -> Unit)? = null
    var onSeekStart: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onSeekEnd: (() -> Unit)? = null

    /** Callback для перемещения внешнего thumb: (x, y) в координатах этого View. */
    var onThumbPosition: ((Float, Float) -> Unit)? = null

    private var isDragging = false

    fun setProgress(p: Float) {
        progress = p.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        invalidate()
        notifyThumbPosition()
    }

    fun setBuffered(b: Float) {
        buffered = b.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        invalidate()
    }

    fun setExpanded(expand: Boolean) {
        expanded = expand
        invalidate()
    }

    fun setCornersEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        if (cornersEnabled == enabled) return
        cornersEnabled = enabled
        invalidate()
    }

    override fun onMeasure(widthMeasureSpec: Int, heightMeasureSpec: Int) {
        val desiredHeight = touchAreaPx.toInt()
        val h = resolveSize(desiredHeight, heightMeasureSpec)
        val w = resolveSize(suggestedMinimumWidth, widthMeasureSpec)
        setMeasuredDimension(w, h)
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)

        val w = width.toFloat()
        val h = height.toFloat()

        val barH = if (expanded) thickHeightPx else thinHeightPx

        // Полоса в самом низу View
        val barBottom = h
        val barTop = h - barH

        val r = if (cornersEnabled) cornerPx else 0f

        // Фон полосы
        tmpRect.set(0f, barTop, w, barBottom)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(tmpRect, r, r, bgPaint)

        // Прогресс
        val progressW = w * progress
        if (progressW > 0f) {
            tmpRect.set(0f, barTop, progressW, barBottom)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(tmpRect, r, r, progressPaint)
        }

        // Thumb рисуется ВНЕШНИМ View, но координаты сообщаем отсюда
        notifyThumbPosition()
    }

    private fun notifyThumbPosition() {
        if (width == 0 || height == 0) return
        val barH = if (expanded) thickHeightPx else thinHeightPx
        val barBottom = height.toFloat()
        val barTop = barBottom - barH
        val cx = (width * progress).coerceIn(0f, width.toFloat())
        // Центр thumb на полосе
        val cy = barTop + barH / 2f
        onThumbPosition?.invoke(cx, cy)
    }

    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        when (event.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                isDragging = true
                parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(true)
                onSeekStart?.invoke()
                handleTouch(event.x)
                return true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                if (isDragging) {
                    handleTouch(event.x)
                }
                return true
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                if (isDragging) {
                    isDragging = false
                    parent?.requestDisallowInterceptTouchEvent(false)
                    onSeekEnd?.invoke()
                }
                return true
            }
        }
        return super.onTouchEvent(event)
    }

    private fun handleTouch(x: Float) {
        val p = (x / width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        progress = p
        invalidate()
        notifyThumbPosition()
        onSeek?.invoke(p)
    }

    fun setDragging(dragging: Boolean) {
        isDragging = dragging
    }

    private fun dpToPx(dp: Float): Float =
        dp * resources.displayMetrics.density
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/RippleView.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.animation.ValueAnimator
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View
import android.view.animation.AccelerateDecelerateInterpolator

/**
 * Расширяющийся круг из точки нажатия, затухающий со временем.
 */
class RippleView @JvmOverloads constructor(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null,
    defStyleAttr: Int = 0
) : View(context, attrs, defStyleAttr) {

    private var centerX = 0f
    private var centerY = 0f
    private var radius = 0f
    private var maxRadius = 0f
    private var alpha = 0f

    private val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
        color = Color.WHITE
    }

    private var animator: ValueAnimator? = null

    fun start(touchX: Float, touchY: Float) {
        centerX = touchX
        centerY = touchY
        maxRadius = Math.min(width.toFloat(), height.toFloat()) * 0.6f
        radius = 0f
        alpha = 0.35f

        animator?.cancel()
        animator = ValueAnimator.ofFloat(0f, 1f).apply {
            duration = 800
            interpolator = AccelerateDecelerateInterpolator()
            addUpdateListener { anim ->
                val v = anim.animatedValue as Float
                radius = maxRadius * v
                alpha = 0.35f * (1f - v)
                invalidate()
            }
            start()
        }
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        if (radius <= 0f || alpha <= 0f) return
        paint.alpha = (alpha * 255).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)
        canvas.drawCircle(centerX, centerY, radius, paint)
    }

    override fun onDetachedFromWindow() {
        super.onDetachedFromWindow()
        animator?.cancel()
        animator = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/CommentsPreviewAdapter.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.ViewGroup
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import coil.load
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Comment
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemCommentPreviewBinding
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

class CommentsPreviewAdapter(
    private var items: List<Comment> = emptyList(),
    private val onClick: (Comment) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<CommentsPreviewAdapter.PreviewViewHolder>() {

    companion object {
        private val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).apply {
            timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
        }
    }

    fun setItems(newItems: List<Comment>) {
        items = newItems
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): PreviewViewHolder {
        val binding = ItemCommentPreviewBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return PreviewViewHolder(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: PreviewViewHolder, position: Int) {
        holder.bind(items[position])
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class PreviewViewHolder(private val binding: ItemCommentPreviewBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(comment: Comment) {
            binding.previewAuthor.text = comment.authorName
            binding.previewDate.text = formatShortDate(comment.dateGmt)
            binding.previewText.text = comment.content

            if (comment.authorAvatar.isNotEmpty()) {
                binding.previewAvatar.load(comment.authorAvatar) {
                    crossfade(false)
                    placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                    error(R.color.card_bg)
                }
            }

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(comment) }
        }
    }

    private fun formatShortDate(gmtDate: String): String {
        if (gmtDate.isEmpty()) return ""
        return try {
            val date = synchronized(dateFmt) { dateFmt.parse(gmtDate) } ?: return gmtDate
            val diff = (System.currentTimeMillis() - date.time) / 1000
            when {
                diff < 60 -> "сейчас"
                diff < 3600 -> "${diff / 60}м"
                diff < 86400 -> "${diff / 3600}ч"
                diff < 2_592_000 -> "${diff / 86400}д"
                diff < 31_536_000 -> "${diff / 2_592_000}мес"
                else -> "${diff / 31_536_000}г"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            gmtDate
        }
    }
}

package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View

import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.data.PlaylistCardItem

class PlaylistChoiceAdapter(
    private var items: List<PlaylistCardItem> = emptyList(),
    private var containsIds: Set<Int> = emptySet(),
    private val onClick: (PlaylistCardItem) -> Unit = {}
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<PlaylistChoiceAdapter.VH>() {

    fun setItems(newItems: List<PlaylistCardItem>) {
        items = newItems
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    fun setContainsIds(ids: Set<Int>) {
        containsIds = ids
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): VH {
        val binding = ItemPlaylistChoiceBinding.inflate(
            LayoutInflater.from(parent.context), parent, false
        )
        return VH(binding)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: VH, position: Int) = holder.bind(items[position])

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

    inner class VH(private val binding: ItemPlaylistChoiceBinding) :
        RecyclerView.ViewHolder(binding.root) {

        fun bind(item: PlaylistCardItem) {
            binding.tvTitle.text = item.title
            binding.tvCount.text = "${item.count} видео"

            val isPrivate = item.visibility == "private"
            val isInPlaylist = containsIds.contains(item.id)

            // Если видео уже в этом плейлисте — иконка "замок/папка с галочкой"
            binding.ivFolder.setImageResource(
                when {
                    isInPlaylist -> R.drawable.ic_folder_check
                    isPrivate -> R.drawable.ic_folder_private
                    else -> R.drawable.ic_folder_public
                }
            )

            // Галочка справа — только если видео уже добавлено
            binding.ivCheck.visibility = if (isInPlaylist) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

            binding.root.setOnClickListener { onClick(item) }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/AudioBoost.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.media.audiofx.LoudnessEnhancer
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper

/**
 * Обёртка над LoudnessEnhancer.
 * - Усиливает звук до [targetGainMb] миллибел.
 * - Плавный fade-in с 0 до целевого усиления за [FADE_IN_MS] мс.
 */
class AudioBoost {

    companion object {
        // ×3 по амплитуде ≈ +9.5 дБ ≈ 950 mB
        private const val TARGET_GAIN_MB = 950
        private const val FADE_IN_MS = 1500L
        private const val FADE_STEP_MS = 30L
    }

    private var enhancer: LoudnessEnhancer? = null
    private var currentGainMb = 0
    private var fadeRunnable: Runnable? = null
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    private var targetAudioSessionId: Int = 0

    fun attach(audioSessionId: Int) {
        if (audioSessionId == 0) return
        if (enhancer != null && targetAudioSessionId == audioSessionId) return

        release()
        targetAudioSessionId = audioSessionId

        try {
            enhancer = LoudnessEnhancer(audioSessionId).apply {
                setTargetGain(0)
                enabled = true
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            enhancer = null
        }
    }

    /** Плавно увеличить громкость с 0 до TARGET_GAIN_MB за FADE_IN_MS. */
    fun fadeIn() {
        cancelFade()
        val e = enhancer ?: return

        currentGainMb = 0
        try {
            e.setTargetGain(0)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
        }

        val steps = (FADE_IN_MS / FADE_STEP_MS).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)
        val stepGain = TARGET_GAIN_MB / steps
        var step = 0

        val r = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                val e2 = enhancer ?: return
                step++
                currentGainMb = (currentGainMb + stepGain).coerceAtMost(TARGET_GAIN_MB)
                try {
                    e2.setTargetGain(currentGainMb)
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                }
                if (step < steps) {
                    handler.postDelayed(this, FADE_STEP_MS)
                }
            }
        }
        fadeRunnable = r
        handler.postDelayed(r, FADE_STEP_MS)
    }

    fun setGain(gainMb: Int) {
        cancelFade()
        val e = enhancer ?: return
        currentGainMb = gainMb.coerceIn(0, TARGET_GAIN_MB)
        try {
            e.setTargetGain(currentGainMb)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
        }
    }

    private fun cancelFade() {
        fadeRunnable?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        fadeRunnable = null
    }

    fun release() {
        cancelFade()
        try {
            enhancer?.enabled = false
            enhancer?.release()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
        }
        enhancer = null
        currentGainMb = 0
        targetAudioSessionId = 0
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/RepliesDialogFragment.kt =====
package ru.asmr1.app.player

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.app.Dialog
import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import coil.load
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetBehavior
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialog
import com.google.android.material.bottomsheet.BottomSheetDialogFragment
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.asmr1.app.R
import ru.asmr1.app.auth.AuthManager
import ru.asmr1.app.data.ApiClient
import ru.asmr1.app.data.Comment
import ru.asmr1.app.data.CommentCreateRequest
import ru.asmr1.app.databinding.DialogRepliesBinding
import kotlin.math.abs

class RepliesDialogFragment : BottomSheetDialogFragment() {

    companion object {
        private const val ARG_PARENT = "parent_comment"
        private const val ARG_VIDEO_ID = "video_id"
        private const val DRAG_THRESHOLD = 150f

        fun newInstance(parent: Comment, videoId: Int) = RepliesDialogFragment().apply {
            arguments = Bundle().apply {
                putParcelable(ARG_PARENT, parent)
                putInt(ARG_VIDEO_ID, videoId)
            }
        }
    }

    private var _binding: DialogRepliesBinding? = null
    private val binding get() = _binding!!

    private lateinit var adapter: CommentsAdapter
    private lateinit var authManager: AuthManager

    private var parentComment: Comment? = null
    private var videoId: Int = 0

    private var sheet: View? = null
    private var behavior: BottomSheetBehavior<View>? = null

    private var dragStartY = 0f
    private var dragStartTranslation = 0f
    private var isDragging = false

    override fun onCreateDialog(savedInstanceState: Bundle?): Dialog {
        return BottomSheetDialog(requireContext())
    }

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View {
        _binding = DialogRepliesBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false)
        return binding.root
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)
        authManager = AuthManager(requireContext())

        parentComment = requireArguments().getParcelable(ARG_PARENT)
        videoId = requireArguments().getInt(ARG_VIDEO_ID, 0)

        val parent = parentComment ?: run {
            dismiss()
            return
        }

        setupParentView(parent)
        setupRecycler()
        setupInput()
        setupClose()

        loadReplies(parent.id)
    }

    override fun onStart() {
        super.onStart()

        val dialog = dialog as? BottomSheetDialog ?: return
        val sheetView = dialog.findViewById<View>(
            com.google.android.material.R.id.design_bottom_sheet
        ) ?: return

        sheet = sheetView

        val screenHeight = resources.displayMetrics.heightPixels
        val targetHeight = (screenHeight * 0.85).toInt()

        sheetView.layoutParams = sheetView.layoutParams.apply {
            height = targetHeight
        }

        behavior = BottomSheetBehavior.from(sheetView).apply {
            state = BottomSheetBehavior.STATE_EXPANDED
            isDraggable = false
            isHideable = true
            skipCollapsed = true
            peekHeight = targetHeight
        }

        setupHeaderDrag()
    }

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    private fun setupHeaderDrag() {
        val header = binding.repliesHeader
        val sheetView = sheet ?: return

        header.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            when (event.actionMasked) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    dragStartY = event.rawY
                    dragStartTranslation = sheetView.translationY
                    isDragging = false
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - dragStartY
                    if (!isDragging && abs(dy) > 10f) isDragging = true
                    if (isDragging) {
                        val newTranslation = (dragStartTranslation + dy).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                        sheetView.translationY = newTranslation
                    }
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP,
                MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    val dy = event.rawY - dragStartY
                    if (isDragging && dy > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
                        dismiss()
                    } else {
                        sheetView.animate()
                            .translationY(0f)
                            .setDuration(200)
                            .start()
                    }
                    isDragging = false
                    true
                }
                else -> false
            }
        }
    }

    private fun setupParentView(parent: Comment) {
        val parentBinding = ru.asmr1.app.databinding.ItemCommentBinding.inflate(
            layoutInflater, binding.parentContainer, false
        )

        parentBinding.commentAuthor.text = parent.authorName
        parentBinding.commentDate.text = ""
        parentBinding.commentText.text = parent.content
        parentBinding.commentLikeCount.text = parent.likes.toString()

        if (parent.authorAvatar.isNotEmpty()) {
            parentBinding.commentAvatar.load(parent.authorAvatar) {
                placeholder(R.color.card_bg)
                error(R.color.card_bg)
            }
        }

        parentBinding.commentShowRepliesRow.visibility = View.GONE
        parentBinding.commentReplyBtn.visibility = View.GONE

        binding.parentContainer.removeAllViews()
        binding.parentContainer.addView(parentBinding.root)
    }

    private fun setupRecycler() {
        adapter = CommentsAdapter(
            onLikeClick = { comment -> onLikeClick(comment) },
            onReplyClick = { comment ->
                binding.replyInput.hint = "Ответить ${comment.authorName}..."
                binding.replyInput.requestFocus()
                val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(
                    requireContext(),
                    InputMethodManager::class.java
                )
                imm?.showSoftInput(binding.replyInput, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
            },
            onShowRepliesClick = { }
        )
        binding.repliesRecycler.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())
        binding.repliesRecycler.adapter = adapter
        binding.repliesRecycler.setHasFixedSize(false)
        binding.repliesRecycler.itemAnimator = null

        // Свайп вправо = Ответить
        binding.repliesRecycler.addOnItemTouchListener(
            SwipeReplyHelper(binding.repliesRecycler) { position ->
                val comment = adapter.getCommentAt(position)
                if (comment != null) {
                    binding.replyInput.hint = "Ответить ${comment.authorName}..."
                    binding.replyInput.requestFocus()
                    val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(
                        requireContext(),
                        InputMethodManager::class.java
                    )
                    imm?.showSoftInput(binding.replyInput, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
                }
            }
        )
    }

    private fun setupInput() {
        binding.replySendBtn.setOnClickListener { sendReply() }
        binding.replyInput.addTextChangedListener(object : android.text.TextWatcher {
            override fun beforeTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, st: Int, c: Int, a: Int) {}
            override fun onTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, st: Int, b: Int, c: Int) {
                val has = !s.isNullOrBlank()
                binding.replySendBtn.isEnabled = has
                binding.replySendBtn.alpha = if (has) 1f else 0.5f
            }
            override fun afterTextChanged(s: android.text.Editable?) {}
        })
        binding.replySendBtn.isEnabled = false
        binding.replySendBtn.alpha = 0.5f
    }

    private fun setupClose() {
        binding.repliesClose.setOnClickListener { dismiss() }
    }

    private fun loadReplies(parentId: Int) {
        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken()
                val api = if (token != null) {
                    ApiClient.createJwtService(token)
                } else {
                    ApiClient.service
                }
                val response = api.getCommentReplies(parentId, page = 1, perPage = 100)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    adapter.setComments(response.replies)
                    binding.repliesEmpty.visibility =
                        if (response.replies.isEmpty()) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
                    binding.repliesRecycler.visibility =
                        if (response.replies.isEmpty()) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
                } else {
                    binding.repliesEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    binding.repliesRecycler.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                binding.repliesEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                binding.repliesRecycler.visibility = View.GONE
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка загрузки", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun sendReply() {
        val text = binding.replyInput.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (text.isEmpty()) return
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы отвечать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }

        val parent = parentComment ?: return

        val draft = Comment(
            id = -System.currentTimeMillis().toInt(),
            content = text,
            authorId = authManager.getUserId(),
            authorName = authManager.getDisplayName() ?: "Вы",
            authorAvatar = "",
            date = "", dateGmt = "",
            likes = 0, liked = false,
            parentId = parent.id,
            repliesCount = 0, replies = emptyList()
        )
        adapter.setComments(adapter.getComments() + draft)
        binding.repliesEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
        binding.repliesRecycler.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        binding.replyInput.text?.clear()
        binding.replySendBtn.isEnabled = false
        binding.replySendBtn.alpha = 0.5f

        val imm = ContextCompat.getSystemService(requireContext(), InputMethodManager::class.java)
        imm?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(binding.replyInput.windowToken, 0)

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                    removeDraft(draft)
                    return@launch
                }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).postComment(
                    videoId,
                    CommentCreateRequest(content = text, parentId = parent.id)
                )
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success && response.comment != null) {
                    val newList = adapter.getComments().map {
                        if (it.id == draft.id) response.comment else it
                    }
                    adapter.setComments(newList)
                } else {
                    removeDraft(draft)
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), response.message ?: "Ошибка", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                removeDraft(draft)
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Ошибка сети", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun removeDraft(draft: Comment) {
        val newList = adapter.getComments().filterNot { it.id == draft.id }
        adapter.setComments(newList)
        if (newList.isEmpty()) {
            binding.repliesEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            binding.repliesRecycler.visibility = View.GONE
        }
    }

    private fun onLikeClick(comment: Comment) {
        if (!authManager.isLoggedIn()) {
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Войдите, чтобы лайкать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }

        val optimistic = comment.copy(
            liked = !comment.liked,
            likes = if (comment.liked) (comment.likes - 1).coerceAtLeast(0) else comment.likes + 1
        )
        adapter.updateComment(optimistic)

        viewLifecycleOwner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            try {
                val token = authManager.getToken() ?: run {
                    adapter.updateComment(comment)
                    return@launch
                }
                val response = ApiClient.createJwtService(token).toggleCommentLike(comment.id)
                if (_binding == null) return@launch

                if (response.success) {
                    adapter.updateComment(comment.copy(likes = response.likes, liked = response.liked))
                } else {
                    adapter.updateComment(comment)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (_binding == null) return@launch
                adapter.updateComment(comment)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView()
        _binding = null
        sheet = null
        behavior = null
    }
}

===== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/ru/asmr1/app/player/ContentFragment.kt =====

class ContentFragment : Fragment() {

    private var likesCount: Int = 0

    private var isDownloading: Boolean = false

    private lateinit var previewAdapter: CommentsPreviewAdapter

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
